*(Interact with marked location)*

**Aglaea:** Phainon, my humble student, my trusted colleague —

> **阿格莱雅：**白厄，我谦逊的学生，信赖的同僚——

**Aglaea:** As I write this, you are locked in a battle of words with the Council of Elders, fighting for the survival of the Flame-Chase Journey on a battlefield where the smoke of war is invisible.

> **阿格莱雅：**在我写下这些文字时，你正与元老院的辩论家唇齿交锋，在硝烟无形的战场上争取逐火之旅的存续。

**Aglaea:** I deeply regret and feel guilty for hastily pushing you and (Trailblazer) into the debate. But please forgive me — at that moment, I could not afford to show any sign of weakness before you, as it would have crushed your morale.

> **阿格莱雅：**对于将你与开拓者仓促推上辩论台的决定，我深感惶恐与愧疚。但请你原谅，彼时，我断然不能在你们面前显露出怯懦——因为那定会打击你们的士气。

**Aglaea:** I considered the debate a routine affair, assuming I could win merely with the support and experience we'd accumulated over centuries. But I underestimated Caenis and her party's cunning, and also the deterioration of my own senses.

> **阿格莱雅：**我本将这场辩论视为惯常之事，以为仅凭在百年岁月中累积的民意与经验便能取胜。但我既低估了凯妮斯和其党羽的狡黠，也低估了自己通感力的退化。

**Aglaea:** The trap was likely set by the debater Callictis. He had prepared a severed golden thread in advance, revealing it to the public when Caenis was faltering in the debate.

> **阿格莱雅：**那陷阱大概是辩手卡勒克提斯设下的。他预先准备好了剪断的金线，在凯妮斯辩论陷入下风时突然将它示于众人面前。

**Aglaea:** He claimed I was reading everyone's thoughts with the golden thread to cheat in the debate. I could have countered that low tactic in a hundred ways, but my words slipped out before I could even think about them...

> **阿格莱雅：**他声称我在用金线阅读众人的思绪，以此在辩论时舞弊。我本有百种方式应对那低级的盘外招，但话语却未经思想的审验便溜出了嘴边……

**Aglaea:** "With deceitful scoundrels like you in the crowd, I have no choice but to rely on the golden thread to keep watch over everything in this holy city."

> **阿格莱雅：**「正因人群中满是如你这般卑鄙的奸宄，我才需以金线监管这圣城中的一切。」

**Aglaea:** You can imagine the reaction that comment provoked. At that moment, I felt my time was running short.

> **阿格莱雅：**那番话语引起的反响，无需我解释你亦能想象。那一刻，我顿觉自己时日无多。

**Aglaea:** The divinity in this body might be eternal — but that is not the essence of "me." I am a daughter of humanity, born of my mother, and I will die as a human.

> **阿格莱雅：**这具躯壳内的神性或许可以永续——但那终究不是「我」的本源。我是人之女，自母亲的胎盘中降生，亦会以人的姿态死去。

**Aglaea:** Realizing that my humanity was fading, I began to plan my exit. What kind of departure would not waste such a belated death? I have yet to find the ideal answer.

> **阿格莱雅：**意识到自己人性将尽之际，我便开始筹划自己的退场。要以怎样的方式离席，才能不浪费这一场迟来的死亡？我一时还未找到最理想的答案。

**Aglaea:** But I knew I couldn't simply fade away in my sleep like anyone else. The serpents have always lurked in the darkness, terrified of their heads being cut off by the golden thread.

> **阿格莱雅：**但我清楚，自己不能与常人一样在睡梦中安然离去。那些毒蛇，它们畏惧被金线割断蛇头，因此才会一直匍匐于黑暗中。

**Aglaea:** If my departure were to be calm and uneventful, it would only lead to a swarm of serpents emerging from their nests. Regrettably, a peaceful death is a luxury I cannot afford.

> **阿格莱雅：**若我的离去平静而无波澜，接踵而至的会是倾巢而出的蛇灾。很遗憾，安享平静的死亡注定是我不可企及的奢侈。

**Aglaea:** I must feign weakness deliberately, to make those serpents lose patience and act rashly. Just as they think they've struck and sunk their fangs into my heels — that's when I'll strike back, igniting the final blaze to burn away the threats hidden in the shadows.

> **阿格莱雅：**我必须主动示弱，引得那些毒蛇失去耐心，蠢蠢欲动。当它们以为自己将要得偿所愿，露出毒牙咬向我的脚踵时——我将以最后的火焰点燃蛇巢，焚烧阴影中的威胁。

**Aglaea:** The blaze soaring into the sky might sow fear and confusion, but I have no worries about people losing their way. After all, Okhema still has you to guide them, Phainon.

> **阿格莱雅：**冲天的火光也许会令人们混乱、畏惧，但我并不担心他们会迷失方向。因为奥赫玛还有你在，白厄。

**Aglaea:** You're not perfect, Phainon. I've witnessed your confusion, recklessness, and deep sensitivity dawn after dawn. The heavy burdens of your past have always weighed you down, inflicting deep pain.

> **阿格莱雅：**你并不完美，白厄。多少个黎明，你的迷茫、鲁莽和多愁善感在我面前上演。沉重的过往压在你的肩上，令你一直痛苦不堪。

**Aglaea:** Yet, in your way, you are perfect. You embrace everyone's imperfections, always ready to find the precious qualities hidden beneath their rough exteriors, unconditionally.

> **阿格莱雅：**但你也是「完美」的。因为你理解并包容所有人的缺憾，你愿意以不设前提的善意鉴出凡人粗坯之下最为珍贵的品质。

**Aglaea:** That might be what sets our sky father Kephale apart from other deities. They recognize the imperfections in their creations yet still choose to shine their light on them, offering protection with unconditional love...

> **阿格莱雅：**我想，那或许也是天父刻法勒与其它神明有别之处。它发现自己造物身上的残缺，却依旧选择照耀他们，以无条件的博爱守护他们……

**Aglaea:** Phainon, while you zealously pursued "Strife," believing it to be your destined path — from the very beginning, my thoughts have never changed.

> **阿格莱雅：**白厄，我想让你明白——在你热忱地追逐「纷争」，以为那才是你应当投身的命运时——从始至终，我的想法从未改变：

**Aglaea:** You are the destined Worldbearer of Amphoreus, the gate to Amphoreus's future.

> **阿格莱雅：**你是翁法罗斯注定的负世者，通向翁法罗斯未来的门扉。

**Aglaea:** Go complete the Flame-Chase Journey of the prophecy. Ignite the stars and inspire hope in the hearts of humanity.

> **阿格莱雅：**去完成神谕中的逐火之旅吧。去点亮星辰，给人子带去希望吧。

**Aglaea:** I will be waiting at the end of the west wind, looking forward to the miracle you will create.

> **阿格莱雅：**我会在西风的尽头，盼待由你开辟出的奇迹。

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Phainon:** Aglaea, how could I possibly be worthy... of the burning torch you've passed into my hands?

> **白厄：**阿格莱雅，我到底何德何能…值得你把炙热的火炬传到我的手上？

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Phainon:** Dan Heng, can you do me a favor?

> **白厄：**丹恒，能请你帮我一个忙吗？

**Dan Heng:** Whatever I can help with, just say the word.

> **丹恒：**我能帮到什么，尽管提吧。

**Phainon:** Please deliver a message to Lord Krateros. Have him gather the Holy City Guards and the Kremnoans to spread the word from door to door: At the Lucid Hour tomorrow, Phainon will speak at the Marmoreal Palace.

> **白厄：**请你为克拉特鲁斯阁下带话，让他集结圣城卫士和悬锋族人，挨家挨户地带去消息：明天的明晰时，白厄会在云石天宫宣讲。

**Phainon:** Urge everyone who has concerns about the holy city's future and the Flame-Chase Journey to attend this gathering. Tell them Aglaea has left Amphoreus the seeds of hope.

> **白厄：**请他们告诉每个人，凡是对圣城的未来和逐火之旅心存顾虑的公民，都应该来参加这次集会。告诉他们，阿格莱雅将希望的种子留给了翁法罗斯。

**Dan Heng:** Understood.

> **丹恒：**好，我明白了。

**Phainon:** Thank you. It's been a harrowing day for everyone. Please make sure to rest well and collect your thoughts. I need to prepare...

> **白厄：**谢谢你。各位都经历了惊魂未定的一天，请务必好好休息，整理一下心情。我还要去做好准……

- ▸ Phainon —
- ▸ Chartonus has arrived.

**Phainon:** ...Chartonus?

> **白厄：**…哈托努斯？

**Phainon:** What brings you to Marmoreal Palace?

> **白厄：**你怎么会来云石天宫？

**Phainon:** I planned to get everything settled before coming to see you. Is there something urgent, Chartonus?

> **白厄：**我本想安顿好一切再去找你。有什么急事吗，哈托努斯？

**Chartonus:** How you're holding up, I wanted to see.

> **哈托努斯：**想要了解，你的情况。

**Chartonus:** Better than I expected, you seem.

> **哈托努斯：**比想象中好些，现在看来。

**Phainon:** There's no room for doubt anymore. It's time to take action and show everyone that the leadership of the Chrysos Heirs is still strong.

> **白厄：**已经没有余地让我犹豫了。必须行动起来，让所有人知道黄金裔的领导没有崩溃。

**Chartonus:** Right, your assessment is. Now, here, take this.

> **哈托努斯：**很正确，你的判断。现在，收下它吧。

**Phainon:** Is this... You've completed it?

> **白厄：**这是…你已经完成了？

**Chartonus:** "Dawnmaker" — the name I've chosen for it.

> **哈托努斯：**「侵晨」，我为它取的名字。

**Chartonus:** Before Dawn, prepare for battle. For tomorrow, wield your sword. Befits your stature, it does, Chrysos Heir.

> **哈托努斯：**备战，于黎明前。挥剑，为了明天。符合你的身份，黄金裔。

**Phainon:** "Dawnmaker"...

> **白厄：**「侵晨」……

**Phainon:** Unbelievable. You've never seen that black-clad swordmaster... yet you've recreated this so precisely, just from what I described?

> **白厄：**不可思议。你明明没有亲眼见过那名黑衣剑客…只是听我用言语复述，竟然就能还原到如此地步吗？

**Chartonus:** Precise, your description.

> **哈托努斯：**精确，你的形容。

**Chartonus:** Imbued it with the power of Dawn, I already have. Guided by your will, gleam with golden flames, its blade will.

> **哈托努斯：**为它注入黎明之力，我已经。听从你的意念，它的锋刃将附着金焰。

**Chartonus:** Wield it. May triumph always be yours.

> **哈托努斯：**挥舞它，愿你战无不胜。

**Phainon:** Thank you, Chartonus. I will use this sword to protect the beings of Amphoreus.

> **白厄：**谢谢你，哈托努斯——我会用这把长剑守护翁法罗斯的生灵。

**Chartonus:** Another thing, there is...

> **哈托努斯：**另一样东西，还有……

**Phainon:** What's this...

> **白厄：**这又是……

**Chartonus:** Stored here, the remnants of Aglaea's divinity are.

> **哈托努斯：**封存于此，阿格莱雅神性的残留。

**Phainon:** ...!

> **白厄：**……！

**Chartonus:** This was her commission. Her death, she had long foreseen.

> **哈托努斯：**这是她的委托。自己的死亡，她早已预见。

**Phainon:** Can she still hear us?

> **白厄：**她…能听得见我们吗？

**Chartonus:** It's difficult to confirm. About divinity, we know very little.

> **哈托努斯：**我难以确认。我们知之甚少，有关神性。

**Chartonus:** In this way, she wanted to be with you, always.

> **哈托努斯：**以此种方式，她想陪伴你们，无论如何。

**Phainon:** I understand. Let me keep it. With this... I'll have another constant companion by my side.

> **白厄：**我明白了，把它交给我保管吧。这样一来…我身上就又多了一件形影不离之物。

**Phainon:** "May this world never have need for a Deliverer." Someone told me long ago.

> **白厄：**很久以前，有人对我说过：「希望这个世界永远都不需要救世主」。

**Phainon:** But when the world dashed her hopes and fell into disarray, if she could see me now — leading from the front, rallying people against their destiny...

> **白厄：**但当世界辜负了她的期望、分崩离析的时候，如果看到是我站在人群的前方，带领人们反抗命运……

**Phainon:** ...She would surely smile.

> **白厄：**…她一定会露出微笑的。

**Pessimistic Citizen:** How will the holy city continue without Lady Goldweaver...

> **消极的公民：**没有「金织」女士，圣城该如何延续……

**Uneasy Citizen:** ...Look! Lord Phainon is here.

> **不安的公民：**…看，白厄阁下来了。

**Hesitant Citizen:** Will he step up to lead Okhema?

> **犹疑的公民：**他会站出来引领奥赫玛么？

**Phainon:** Not long ago, I spoke at the citizens' assembly at Dawncloud.

> **白厄：**不久以前，我在黎明云崖的公民大会上发表了一番讲话。

**Phainon:** I wrestled with every word, searching for the right ones to rally support and keep the Chrysos Heirs' Flame-Chase alive.

> **白厄：**那时，为了延续黄金裔逐火的希望，我奋力地思考该用什么样的话术博取人们的支持。

**Phainon:** In the end, I remembered a lesson from one of my teachers: In battles where words are weapons, outrage and blind passion often overpower reason.

> **白厄：**最后，我想起了一位老师的教导：在以口舌为兵器的场合，激愤和盲目是远胜理性的强大武器。

**Phainon:** I followed his strategy. I painted my opponents as nothing more than pests, tore apart their character, and laid out their crimes.

> **白厄：**所以我采取了他的策略。我将站在对立面的论敌贬低为虫豸，抨击他们的品格，并列数他们的罪行。

**Phainon:** The strategy worked. I secured enough votes to achieve my goal. But afterward? There wasn't even a flicker of joy.

> **白厄：**我的策略奏效了。它为我争取到了足够多的票数，让我达成了想要的目标。但事后？我没有感到分毫的喜悦。

**Phainon:** I realized — that apparent victory masked a deeper failure. In my pursuit of success, I fueled division and strayed far from the very purpose that led me to the Flame-Chase Journey.

> **白厄：**因为我很清楚——那场表面上的胜利掩盖了更大的失败。在我为了胜利不顾手段地挑唆对立的情绪时，我已经和自己加入逐火事业的初衷背道而驰。

**Phainon:** This journey was supposed to unify, not divide. To strengthen us, not weaken us.

> **白厄：**这段征途本应凝聚，而非分裂；它本应令我们强大，而非使我们脆弱。

**Phainon:** It was the gold thread that mended our rifts in the past. Some saw it as a means of surveillance, a tool of control, but no one can deny that it was Aglaea who held this city, and even this shattered world, together.

> **白厄：**过去，是金色的丝线在为我们缝补裂痕。有人会将它比作监视的眼线，掌控的工具，但没人能够否认…是阿格莱雅将这座城市，乃至这个支离破碎的世界缝缀在了一起。

**Phainon:** Now she is gone — yes, as you have heard — the demigod Aglaea, who guarded the holy city for a millennium, has indeed fallen.

> **白厄：**但如今她已不在——没错，正如你们听闻的那样——守护圣城千年的半神阿格莱雅，的确已经陨落了。

**Furious Citizen:** Who murdered Lady Goldweaver?

> **激愤的公民：**是谁谋害了金织女士？

**Righteous Citizen:** Find the murderer! Make them pay!

> **正义的公民：**找到凶手！严惩他们！

**Phainon:** I felt the same as all of you initially.

> **白厄：**起初，我的心情也和你们一样。

**Phainon:** I wanted to stand here beside you, to condemn the sinners, to discuss retribution, and to see justice brought down upon them...

> **白厄：**起初，我也想在这里与你们一同痛斥罪人的恶行，商议该如何追讨他们，在他们的头顶降下惩罚……

**Phainon:** ...But my perspective has changed.

> **白厄：**…但我的想法改变了。

**Phainon:** The golden threads that once bound this city have unraveled, yet no one is celebrating their freedom from control. In the empty markets and bath chambers, one feeling lingers: Unease.

> **白厄：**此刻，城中的金线已经散去，我却没有看到任何人在为脱离掌控雀跃欢呼。在空荡的市集和浴宫，我只把一种情绪收入眼中：不安。

**Phainon:** Deep down, we all know — the last safeguard against the harm we may do to each other is gone. No divine power will protect us from the malice in our hearts. From now on, only the moral code we choose to uphold for ourselves will stand between us and the abyss.

> **白厄：**因为在场的每个人都心知肚明，防止我们被彼此心底阴暗所伤的最后保障已经消失。从此，除了我们为自己捏塑的道德律令，再也没有哪股神力能替我们抵御恶意。

**Mem:** Phainon has a way with words.

> **迷迷：**白厄他，好会说呀。

- ▸ Be quiet, and listen carefully.

**Mem:** ...Oh! Alright.

> **迷迷：**…哦！好吧。

- ▸ There's something special about a man of letters.

**Mem:** Indeed!

- ▸ If it were me up there, I could do it too.

**Mem:** ...You think highly of yourself, don't you?

> **迷迷：**…你还真是自信呀？

**Phainon:** The Flame-Chase Journey is nearing its end, with only one Titan left for the Chrysos Heirs to conquer: Aquila.

> **白厄：**逐火之旅已经接近尾声，黄金裔唯一需要征服的泰坦仅剩艾格勒一尊。

**Phainon:** Before I rise to the skies, I challenge you — cast out the shadows and malice within your hearts. Let your spirits shine brighter than the sky itself!

> **白厄：**可在登临天空以前，我会先向你们发起挑战——我挑战你们心中可能的阴仄与恶意，让你们的心灵先于这片天空放晴！

**Phainon:** Let me bear witness: Even without the divine authority of Romance, Okhema will remain a sanctuary in these end times. A place for the kind people Aglaea dreamed of, the kind of people foretold in prophecy...

> **白厄：**请允许我见证：哪怕失去了「浪漫」的神权，奥赫玛仍会是末世中的理想乡。在这里，居住着阿格莱雅所期待的、神谕中的新世界所期待的……

**Phainon:** A better people!

> **白厄：**更好的人！

**Spirited Citizen:** ...Better people!

> **振奋的公民：**…更好的人！

**Inspired Citizens:** Better people! Better people!

> **鼓舞的公民们：**更好的人！更好的人！

**Okheman:** Better people! Better people! Better people!

> **奥赫玛人：**更好的人！更好的人！更好的人！

**Phainon:** I appreciate your willingness to accept the challenge... You have reminded me that Aglaea left me perhaps the simplest duty in the world.

> **白厄：**你们愿意接受挑战…我心存感激。你们令我醒悟：阿格莱雅留给我的或许是世上最质朴的职责。

**Phainon:** To bear the burden of creation, I need only to give one thing. It's a gift she once gave me without hesitation, and now I share it with each of you — unshakable faith.

> **白厄：**要背负创世的命运，我只需付出一样东西。那是她曾慷慨地赠予我的礼物，而我也会转交给你们每个人：毫无保留的深信。

**Phainon:** There's still a long way to go before Amphoreus is reborn. But we will not forget the past as we move forward, for the memories of Amphoreus will always benefit future generations.

> **白厄：**距离翁法罗斯的新生还有一段长路。但我们不会在前进时遗忘过去，因为翁法罗斯的记忆总能造福后人。

**Phainon:** Allow me this moment. I want to borrow a few minutes of your time to commemorate a demigod who protected the holy city for a millennium...

> **白厄：**请将这一刻借给我吧。我想征用各位几分钟的光阴，一起缅怀一位庇佑圣城千年的半神……

**Phainon:** ...She was Okhema's dressmaker, the leader of the Chrysos Heirs, the Goldweaver Aglaea. And her greatest feat —

> **白厄：**…她是奥赫玛的改衣师，黄金裔的领袖，「金织」阿格莱雅。而她这一生最大的壮举——

**Phainon:**— was being a selfless person.

> **白厄：**——是成为一个无私的人。

**Trianne:** That singing sounds so familiar!

> **缇安：**这歌声…好熟悉呀！

**Trianne:** That's our song, and Snowy used to love it!

> **缇安：**我们的歌谣，小白以前最喜欢啦！

**Phainon:** Yes, Lady Trianne. For that child filled with anxiety...

> **白厄：**是啊，缇安老师。对于那个心里充满不安的孩子……

**Phainon:** ...your songs always dispelled the clouds in my heart, even if just for a moment.

> **白厄：**…你们的歌谣总能驱散我心里的阴云，哪怕只有短短的一刻。

**Trianne:** Hehe... Snowy was so cute and kind when he was little!

> **缇安：**嘿嘿…小时候的小白，很可爱，很善良！

**Trianne:** Now... hmm, you've grown so much. Maybe not as cute but still just as kind!

> **缇安：**现在…唔，变得好大一只，没那么可爱了？但还是很善良哇！

**Phainon:** Hahaha... Time spares no one, so there's nothing we can do. But kindness... you helped nurture it in me.

> **白厄：**哈哈哈…岁月催人老，这谁都没办法。但善意…是你们帮我滋养了它。

**Trianne:** Don't mention it! Your light is as bright as ever. Keep it up and stay strong!

> **缇安：**不用谢啦！你的光芒，很耀眼！继续，保持！

**Trianne:** See you, Snowy.

> **缇安：**再见啦，小白~

**Phainon:** Goodbye, Lady Trianne.

> **白厄：**再见，缇安老师。

**Anaxa:** I heard what you said. Are you suggesting the debate tactics I've taught you are nothing but sleight of hand?

> **那刻夏：**听你刚才那番话，是觉得我教给你的辩论术是邪门歪道么？

**Phainon:** Not at all, Professor Anaxa. Honestly, without those techniques you taught me, the Flame-Chase Journey might not have survived.

> **白厄：**我可没那么说啊，那刻夏老师。毕竟要是没有你传授给我的技巧，逐火之旅还存不存在都两说了。

**Anaxa:** Hmph... Good thing your head's still clear.

> **那刻夏：**哼…还好你脑子足够清晰。

**Anaxa:** The Chrysos Heir of Kephale... I had no idea you had it in you while I was drilling those formulas into your head in class.

> **那刻夏：**对应刻法勒的黄金裔么…呵，当初在教室里按着你的脑袋背公式的时候，可真没想到你还是这块料。

**Phainon:** Who could have thought, Professor? By the way, I've completely forgotten those formulas now.

> **白厄：**谁能想得到呢，老师？顺带一说，那些公式我现在也已经忘得一干二净了。

**Anaxa:** Ha, some things never change. In our next lives, I'll teach you how to truly embrace your divine duties.

> **那刻夏：**呵，真是不可教也。来世，让我好好教你该如何承担神职吧。

**Phainon:** Hahaha... I'll be waiting.

> **白厄：**哈哈哈…那我等着。

**Phainon:** Goodbye, Professor Anaxa.

> **白厄：**再见，那刻夏老师。

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Phainon:** ...Miss Castorice?

> **白厄：**…遐蝶小姐？

**Phainon:** Aren't you going to say something?

> **白厄：**你…不说些什么吗？

**Castorice:** Lord Phainon, you... have always been so approachable.

> **遐蝶：**白厄阁下，你…总是那么平易近人。

**Castorice:** Ever since we first met, you've seen me as a friend... It was the first time I understood what everyday friendship was like.

> **遐蝶：**自我们认识起，你就一直将我视作伙伴…我在你身上第一次体会到了什么是普通人间的友谊。

**Castorice:** But that's... dangerous.

> **遐蝶：**但那…也很危险。

**Phainon:** Dangerous?

> **白厄：**危险？

**Castorice:** Laughing and chatting with you, I sometimes forget who I am... and for someone with my kind of power, that's a real hazard.

> **遐蝶：**因为和你一起说笑时，我偶尔会忘记自己是谁…对于拥有我这般力量的人，那非常危险。

**Phainon:** Ah... I hadn't thought of it that way. Sorry, I forgot to consider your perspective.

> **白厄：**啊…原来还有这一层意思。抱歉，我忘记换位思考了。

**Castorice:** Please, no apologies... Despite everything, thinking back on the times we spent together makes me really happy.

> **遐蝶：**请别道歉…因为尽管如此，那些我们一起度过的时光…回想起来还是十分令我开心。

**Phainon:** ...I feel the same.

> **白厄：**…我又何尝不是呢。

**Castorice:** Goodbye, Phainon.

> **遐蝶：**再见了，白厄。

**Phainon:** Goodbye, Castorice.

> **白厄：**再见，遐蝶。

**Aglaea:** Hehe...

> **阿格莱雅：**呵……

**Phainon:** ...Aglaea.

> **白厄：**…阿格莱雅。

**Aglaea:** Remember, Phainon... after completing the Flame-Chase, leave the decision to initiate the Miracle of Genesis to the Amphoreans.

> **阿格莱雅：**要记得，白厄…完成逐火以后，要把开启创世奇迹的决定交由翁法罗斯的人们。

**Aglaea:** The Chrysos Heirs are the executors of the prophecy, yet they — the people are the true masters of this world.

> **阿格莱雅：**黄金裔是神谕的代行者，而他们——他们才是这个世界的主人。

**Phainon:** Rest assured. I always keep your teachings in mind.

> **白厄：**放心吧。我一直记得你的教诲——

**Phainon:** "The difference between a presumptuous hero and a tyrant is a fine line."

> **白厄：**「自命不凡的英雄，与桀恶仅有一线之隔」，对么？

**Aglaea:** Well said. You've got a good memory.

> **阿格莱雅：**你记得很清楚…这很好。

**Phainon:** It's late... Anything else you'd like to add?

> **白厄：**已经这个时候了…你没有什么别的话想说吗？

**Aglaea:** Anything else...?

> **阿格莱雅：**别的话么……

**Aglaea:** Even in your imagination... I'm not one to show my vulnerable side.

> **阿格莱雅：**哪怕只是在你的想象中…我也不擅表露自己脆弱的一面啊。

**Phainon:** Ha... no worries. I've long understood how difficult it is for you to open up completely.

> **白厄：**哈…没关系，让你说出一句毫无遮掩的交心话到底有多难，这我早就知道了。

**Phainon:** Thank you for bringing me here, Aglaea... You saved me.

> **白厄：**谢谢你把我带到了这里，阿格莱雅…你拯救了我。

**Aglaea:** Heh... maybe what I wanted to save wasn't you, child...

> **阿格莱雅：**呵…也许我想要拯救的不是你，孩子……

**Aglaea:** ...but this crumbling world.

> **阿格莱雅：**…而是这个摇摇欲坠的世界。

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Aglaea:** Goodbye, Phainon.

> **阿格莱雅：**再见，白厄。

**Phainon:** Goodbye, Aglaea.

> **白厄：**再见，阿格莱雅。

**Mydei:** Hmph... Did you forget me?

> **万敌：**哼…忘了谁了？

**Phainon:** Ha, forget you? Like I'd ever.

> **白厄：**哈，忘了你？那怎么可能。

**Phainon:** We aren't parting ways forever. You're just off to guard the gates at Castrum Kremnos. Let's not make this a tearful farewell.

> **白厄：**咱们之间又不是生离死别，你只是去悬锋城看门了而已，就别掺和这么催人泪下的场合了。

**Mydei:** Heh... you know just how to make a person uncomfortable.

> **万敌：**呵…你还是那么懂得如何令人不快啊。

**Mydei:** I have a feeling we'll meet again in this lifetime. Let's have another showdown then, "Deliverer."

> **万敌：**我有预感，你我在此世还会再见。有机会时再较量一番吧，「救世主」。

**Phainon:** That's very confident of you. I've changed since then. Have you seen this sword? It's a masterpiece, freshly forged by Chartonus.

> **白厄：**这么自信？现在的我可不比以往了。看见这把剑了吗？这可是刚出哈托努斯锻炉的极品。

**Mydei:** Ha... A bold statement for a mere mortal who hasn't yet claimed the power of a Titan.

> **万敌：**哈…尚未夺得泰坦神权的区区凡夫，口气倒是狂妄得很。

**Mydei:** See you around, Phainon of Aedes Elysiae.

> **万敌：**回头见，哀丽秘榭的白厄。

**Phainon:** It's a date, Mydeimos.

> **白厄：**约好了，迈德漠斯。

**???:** ...What a poignant, yet splendid journey.

> **？？？：**…真是一段感伤，却又精彩的旅途呀。

**???:** And it's still not over yet... is it?

> **？？？：**而且还没结束…对吗？

**Phainon:** Yes, Cyrene.

> **白厄：**对，昔涟。

**Cyrene:** I was right. In the end, you did become the Deliverer in this story.

> **昔涟：**果然还是被我说中了吧？最后的最后，你还是成为了这个故事里的救世主呀。

**Phainon:** A lot has changed while you were away. I've met so many people and tried so many things. I've found new resolve... and let go of some attachments.

> **白厄：**你不在的时间里…很多事都变了。我认识了各种各样的人，也做了很多不同的事。我拾起了一些决心…也放下了一些执念。

**Phainon:** I'm not the same person I used to be.

> **白厄：**我改变了很多。

**Cyrene:** Hee...

> **昔涟：**嘻……

**Cyrene:** But to me —

> **昔涟：**但是，在我看来——

**Cyrene:** You've never changed at all, Phainon.

> **昔涟：**你从来都没有变哦，白厄。

Days later, at Dawncloud...

**Cipher:** Ah, Dawncloud... This place holds so many different kinds of memories.

> **赛飞儿：**呵，黎明云崖…我对这里的记忆还真是百味交杂。

**Cipher:** I remember you could see the "Goldweaver" sign from here, all the way down the mountain.

> **赛飞儿：**还记得，那个时候…从这里往山下望，一眼就能辨认出「金织」的招牌。

**Cipher:** But my eyes aren't what they used to be a thousand years ago...

> **赛飞儿：**一千年过去，我的眼神也不如从前那么锐利了哪……

**Cipher:** ...

> **赛飞儿：**……

**Aglaea:** Cifera, you...

> **阿格莱雅：**赛法利娅，你……

**Cipher:** Seamstress! Tell me!

> **赛飞儿：**裁缝女，我问你！

**Cipher:** Does Okhema have any treasures so rare that not even your... I mean, our shop, has seen?

> **赛飞儿：**奥赫玛有没有什么稀罕的宝贝，是连你…唔…连我们的店里都没有的？

**Aglaea:** ...

> **阿格莱雅：**……

**Aglaea:** Of course, it has. I heard there's a "Gem of All Worlds" hidden somewhere in Dawncloud. Apparently it's a piece that fell out of the Dawn Device.

> **阿格莱雅：**当然。我听闻，在黎明云崖的某处藏着一颗「全世宝石」，传说它是从黎明机器上脱落的碎屑。

**Aglaea:** It's truly a priceless treasure, being a part of Kephale's divine body. Even I, the Goldweaver, cannot witness its glamour.

> **阿格莱雅：**作为刻法勒神躯的一部分，它是真正的无价之宝，即便「金织」也无从染指它的珠光。

**Cipher:** "Gem of All Worlds"...

> **赛飞儿：**「全世宝石」……

**Cipher:** ...Nice! Got it! Well, please keep looking after the shop while I—

> **赛飞儿：**…哈，我记下啦！那你接着忙店里的事，我先……

**Aglaea:** ...Let me guess, Cifera.

> **阿格莱雅：**…让我猜猜你在想什么吧，赛法利娅。

**Aglaea:** You're probably already plotting to sneak into Dawncloud to steal that gem, aren't you?

> **阿格莱雅：**你大概已经在盘算要如何混入黎明云崖，将那颗宝石收入囊中了，对么？

**Cipher:** ...Erm.

> **赛飞儿：**…唔。

**Aglaea:** And your next move is probably trying to sell it to the highest bidder, then distribute that money to the poor and the refugees in the holy city.

> **阿格莱雅：**你大概还想到了下一步：要把它卖出一个好价钱，然后把得来的财富分给圣城的穷人、难民……

**Cipher:** ...Look, seamstress. Are you sure you aren't peeking into my head with your golden threads?

> **赛飞儿：**…裁缝女，你真的没在用金线偷看我的想法嘛？

**Aglaea:** You might be skilled at covering up lies, but your compassion is all over your face, plain as day.

> **阿格莱雅：**你擅长的只是掩饰谎言。可你的同情心，一直跃然脸上。

**Cipher:** ...

> **赛飞儿：**……

**Aglaea:** Don't lie to yourself. Do you honestly think this is the right thing to do, Cifera?

> **阿格莱雅：**不要蒙蔽自己。打你的心底…你认为这样做是正确的吗，赛法利娅？

**Cipher:** ...What else am I supposed to do? That's the only thing I can do with my power!

> **赛飞儿：**…那我还能怎么办？以我的力量能做到的…就只有这些而已。

**Cipher:** You keep talking about how I have the potential to become a good person, or even what people call a "hero"...

> **赛飞儿：**裁缝女，你常说，我有成为一个好人，甚至是成为大家眼中的英雄的潜质……

**Cipher:** But I don't know what I need to do to get there... I can't weave golden dresses like you, I can't sing as well as Miss Hysilens, and I definitely don't know as much as big sis Tribbie...

> **赛飞儿：**但我不知道该怎么做…我没法像你那样织出金光闪闪的布匹，也没有海瑟音姐姐那么美妙的歌喉，更不可能像缇宝阿姐那样知识渊博……

**Cipher:** The only things I'm good at are the underhanded street smarts that no one even respects!

> **赛飞儿：**我唯一擅长的东西…就只有在街头领悟的，那些在大家眼里见不得光的活计而已！

**Cipher:** But... but I still want to be worthy of you. I want to prove that you've made the right choice. Even thieves can dream, right...?

> **赛飞儿：**可即便如此，我也想…想配得上你的选择。小偷也有做梦的权利，对吧……

**Cipher:** Tsk...

> **赛飞儿：**嘁……

**Aglaea:** ...

> **阿格莱雅：**……

**Aglaea:** I know, Cifera... I know.

> **阿格莱雅：**我明白，赛法利娅…我明白。

**Aglaea:** You are justified in your anger. Destiny is never fair, and your path is doomed to be even worse than everyone else's. You are fated to walk in the dark and bear the world's misunderstanding in this journey.

> **阿格莱雅：**你有千万个理由控诉，因为宿命就是如此不公。你要走的道路注定更为曲折…在这趟旅程中，你势必要在背光处承受人们的误解。

**Aglaea:** But you have to believe that everyone in this world is unique in their own way.

> **阿格莱雅：**但你要相信一件事：每个人都是世上独一无二的存在。

**Aglaea:** I am convinced that you're the only one who can bear the authority of Trickery, even if you have to contend with the legendary three hundred master thieves of Dolos.

> **阿格莱雅：**即便算上多洛斯传说中的三百侠盗…我依然坚信，你会是唯一能够承载「诡计」神职的命运之女。

**Cipher:** A–Aglaea...

> **赛飞儿：**阿…阿格莱雅……

**Cipher:** I just want everyone to believe that the world is beautiful and that everyone is simple and kind... If only I could get everyone to believe that...

> **赛飞儿：**要是能让所有人都相信我就好了…我想让大家都认为，世界是个美好的地方…世上的人们都很单纯善良。

**Cipher:** Would that lie become reality if enough people believed in it? ...Even though it's a lie?

> **赛飞儿：**哪怕我知道那是假话，但只要相信的人足够多…谎言是不是就能成真…？

**Aglaea:** ...

> **阿格莱雅：**……

**Aglaea:** Lies will always be lies, Cifera.

> **阿格莱雅：**谎言永远都是谎言，赛法利娅……

**Aglaea:** But sometimes... they can be more noble than the truth.

> **阿格莱雅：**只是，在某些时候…它的确可以比坦诚高贵。

**Cipher:** ...

> **赛飞儿：**……

**Cipher:** ...Are you really planning to just stand there forever? Honestly, sneaking around doesn't suit you, Deliverer.

> **赛飞儿：**…你打算就一直在那儿站着？恕我直言，鬼鬼祟祟的风格可不适合你——救世小子。

**Phainon:** Ahaha... I never thought I could slip past your sharp ears, Miss Cipher. I just didn't think it'd be right to interrupt when you're obviously lost in thoughts.

> **白厄：**哈哈哈…我可没想瞒过你的耳朵，赛飞儿小姐。我只是隐隐感觉到你被思绪缠住了，不想贸然打扰。

**Cipher:** Hmph. Still as charming with your words as ever, boy. Your speech at the public baths wasn't half bad, I'll admit. It managed to touch my heart just a smidge.

> **赛飞儿：**哈…小子，说话还是这么招人待见哪。你之前在大浴场的宣讲，还不赖。我承认，自己有那么一丢丢被触动到。

**Phainon:** Thank you. I had a feeling back then that you were in the audience. Intuition certainly is a thing of wonder.

> **白厄：**多谢夸奖。直觉是样神奇的东西，当时我就隐约感觉到，你也在场。

**Cipher:** ...Oh? And you didn't call me out on the spot? You waited all this time to tell me?

> **赛飞儿：**…哦？那你怎么没当时就把我叫出来，居然还等到现在？

**Phainon:** Well... I thought you'd need some time to yourself... after she passed away.

> **白厄：**因为…我猜你也需要一段时间来整理好心情，悼念她的离开。

**Cipher:** ...

> **赛飞儿：**……

**Cipher:** Hm. None of your business.

> **赛飞儿：**哼，多管闲事。

**Cipher:** Enough small talk. Tell me, what kind of trouble did you run into that you need me, the super-old-timer, to step in?

> **赛飞儿：**寒暄到此为止。说吧，你遇上什么麻烦了，需要我这个大前辈帮忙？

**Phainon:** We've only met a couple of times, but you've always gone straight to business. I respect that about you, "old-timer," so I'll cut to the chase.

> **白厄：**你我一共就见过几次面，每次都是直奔主题…我欣赏你的直爽，「前辈」——那我就有话直说了。

**Phainon:** You're probably aware that Aglaea has earned the support of the entire city for the Chrysos Heirs. Everyone believes that she died at the hands of Council members who are against the Flame-Chase, and the citizens' fury has been dragging these schemers out of the shadows one by one.

> **白厄：**如你所见，阿格莱雅为黄金裔挣得了全城公民的支持。人们一致认为是元老院中反对逐火的派别谋害了她，公民的激愤让藏在阴影中的险恶无处可躲。

**Phainon:** But they didn't get everybody. To this day, we still haven't found Elder Caenis — the leader of the "Cleaners."

> **白厄：**可即便如此，警报也没有彻底解除。「清洗者」的领袖，元老凯妮斯——我们至今没能找到她的下落。

**Cipher:** Ah. I see. You're afraid she'll do something wild now that she's pushed into a corner, aren't you?

> **赛飞儿：**噢，我明白了。你怕她会狗急跳墙，对吧？

**Phainon:** Yes. I believe that Kephale's Coreflame, forever enshrined above the council theater, would be the opposition's final trump card against the Chrysos Heirs.

> **白厄：**是。刻法勒的火种一直被供奉在议会剧场的上空，我想它会是反对者们阻挠黄金裔的最后底牌。

**Phainon:** We're setting out to campaign against Aquila in a few days. With the Chrysos Heirs gone, it'll be their best chance to strike...

> **白厄：**再过几日，我们就要启程征讨艾格勒了。黄金裔远离圣城的这段时间，是他们下手的最佳时机……

**Cipher:** You think those desperate dogs will try to snatch the Coreflame while you're gone —

> **赛飞儿：**你害怕那些落魄的野狗会趁你们离开的时候偷走火种——

**Cipher:**— So you want me to give them a nice long walk while you're away, right?

> **赛飞儿：**——所以你想找我帮忙，在你缺席的时间里遛遛他们，对么？

**Phainon:** You sure saw right through me, Miss Cipher. I know exactly what you can do. God-like speed, perfect disguises, untraceable movements... And that most enigmatic authority of "Trickery."

> **白厄：**每一点都被押中了，不愧是你。我清楚你的力量，赛飞儿小姐：神速、伪装、来去无形…还有那最让人捉摸不透的，「欺骗」的权柄。

**Phainon:** I have only one request. Please, use whatever tricks you need to protect Kephale's Coreflame, even if you have to deceive the rest of the Chrysos Heirs as well.

> **白厄：**请尽管动用你的手段，哪怕要把我们也连带着一起蒙蔽。我只有一个请求：请保护好刻法勒的火种。

**Cipher:** Uh-huh...

> **赛飞儿：**嗯哼……

**Cipher:** Hey, Deliverer. Do you honestly think that there's no way I'll turn you down?

> **赛飞儿：**我说，救世小子，你是不是打心底觉得…我压根没有拒绝你的可能？

**Cipher:** Do you even have any Plan Bs if I decide to nope out on this?

> **赛飞儿：**要是我不打算管这摊子麻烦事，你还有什么后备计划？

**Phainon:** As the demigod of Trickery, there are no other trump cards in the world that can out-rank you — I'm sure you know the answer to your own question, Miss Cipher.

> **白厄：**身为「诡计」的半神，恐怕这世界上没有比你更具威力的底牌了——你认为呢，赛飞儿小姐？

**Cipher:** What confidence. You talk as if you have everything in the palm of your hand... Hmph, so you did learn a thing or two from her. I'll give you that.

> **赛飞儿：**一副掌控全局的自信啊…你算是学到了半点她的精髓。

**Cipher:** Then let me tell you something you don't know. The Flame Reaver — they've been watching Okhema closely.

> **赛飞儿：**那就来说些你不知道的事吧。「盗火行者」——据我观察，那家伙可是对奥赫玛虎视眈眈哪。

**Phainon:** You mean...

> **白厄：**你的意思是……

**Cipher:** Bet you didn't expect that, huh? I've been tracking them all this time, and this is my conclusion.

> **赛飞儿：**猜不到吧？我一直盯着那家伙的行踪呢。而我得出的结论……

**Cipher:** That lunatic may have lost their mind, but they're no fool. Why do you think they haven't made a move on Okhema yet?

> **赛飞儿：**那家伙是像条疯狗一样丧失理智，但它绝对不是傻子。你猜它为什么一直没对奥赫玛出手？

**Phainon:** Professor Anaxa once analyzed this. He thought... It was probably because Kephale's Coreflame was protected by the Council of Elders' secret arts?

> **白厄：**那刻夏老师分析过这事。当时的结论…大概是因为刻法勒的火种有元老院的秘法保护？

**Cipher:** Ha! Secret arts my foot. I could break those mechanisms with a flick of my finger.

> **赛飞儿：**哈！什么糠包秘法，我动动手指就能破了它的机关。

**Cipher:** Allow me to let you in on the actual secret. They probably held off from attacking Okhema because the seamstress's defense network was way tougher than you thought.

> **赛飞儿：**告诉你吧，那家伙之所以没有贸然进攻奥赫玛——多半是因为裁缝女织成的防御网比你们想象中的还要坚固。

**Cipher:** Need to make sure that you don't die before you pay me.

**Hyacine:** What about the Daythunder Knight's family, allies, and friends? What about the survivors and those who died in the great battles? Whenever I think about their stories by the campfire, I realize the details of those legends have been long lost... and only Seliose's name remains.

> **风堇：**阳雷骑士的亲人、盟友、伙伴，大战的幸存者、牺牲者…每当我在篝火边思念他们的故事，却总是发现传说的细节早已遗失，只留下塞涅俄丝的名字。

> **先祖之灵：**是谁…唤醒了我？

**Hyacine:** It's I, Hyacinthia, a descendant of the Sky people. I apologize for interrupting your slumber.

> **风堇：**是我，雅辛忒丝，天空一族的后裔。很抱歉打断了您的酣眠。

**Hyacine:** You are Lord Crispus, aren't you? I've read your story in our family tree. You were a righteous judge who vindicated many who were wrongfully accused.

> **风堇：**您是克里斯普斯阁下，对吗？我在族谱上读到过您的故事。听说您曾是一位正直的裁判官，为许多遭受诬告的公民洗刷了冤屈。

**Crispus:** Ah... I never imagined someone in this era would still remember my name.

> **克里斯普斯：**啊…没想到在这个陌生的时代，还能有人记得我的名字。

**Crispus:** So, our bloodline and duty have continued to this distant age...

> **克里斯普斯：**我们的血脉和使命…一直延续到了这遥远的年代啊。

**Hyacine:** Indeed it has. I have awakened you in the hope of receiving your blessing.

> **风堇：**没错。我之所以会叫醒您，就是希望能够取得您的祝福。

**Hyacine:** For the Flame-Chase Journey is nearing its perfect conclusion, and retrieving the Coreflame of Aquila is the final challenge the heroes will face.

> **风堇：**因为逐火之旅将在我的眼前走向完满的结局，而取得艾格勒的火种则是英雄们将要面临的最后一重挑战。

**Crispus:** Perfect conclusion...?

> **克里斯普斯：**完满的结局…？

**Crispus:** Hyacinthia, you look very young... and you do not have the bearings of a warrior who can contend with a Titan.

> **克里斯普斯：**雅辛忒丝，你很年轻…而且，你的模样也不像是能与泰坦抗衡的斗士。

**Crispus:** How can you convince me that this blessing will not turn into a curse, leading a child like you to your demise?

> **克里斯普斯：**你要如何令我确信，这祝福不会变成诅咒，反而铺就一位年轻后嗣的绝路？

**Hyacine:** A thousand years ago, Seliose who conquered the skies left behind legends and a will, entrusting the mission to return the Sky Titan's Coreflame to her descendants — to people like you and me.

> **风堇：**千年以前，征服天空的塞涅俄丝留下了传说和遗志，把归还天空泰坦火种的使命交给了自己的后人——您和我。

**Hyacine:** Though I'm not a warrior, I will not turn away from the mission that has flowed through our blood for a thousand years.

> **风堇：**我虽然不是一名战士，但也绝对不会逃避在血脉中流淌了千年的使命。

**Hyacine:** And I am not fighting alone... I have the strongest and most tenacious companions in the world.

> **风堇：**何况我并非孤军奋战…在我身边，聚集了这世上最强大，最坚韧的伙伴们。

**Hyacine:** Amphoreus is in an era brimming with heroes! The burden of retrieving Aquila's Coreflame no longer rests solely on the shoulders of the chosen Skyborn.

> **风堇：**翁法罗斯已经迎来了一个英雄辈出的时代！取回艾格勒火种的重担，已经不需要由被选中的天空之子独自承担了。

**Crispus:** Mmm...

> **克里斯普斯：**嗯……

**Hyacine:** I now wield the power of healing. I aim to mend the fissures in the sky and heal the wounds of the people.

> **风堇：**我现在掌握了治愈的力量。我想要缝合天空的裂隙，疗愈人们的伤痛。

**Hyacine:** I desire to define the mission of the Skyfolk in my own terms... to conclude the vigil that has spanned millennia.

> **风堇：**我想以自己的意志去诠释天空后裔的使命…以我的方式，为跨越千年的守望画上句号。

**Crispus:** ...

> **克里斯普斯：**……

**Crispus:** I sense resilience in your words... not an outward resilience, but something deeper within.

> **克里斯普斯：**从你的话语里，我感受到了坚韧…并非流于外表，而是包蕴于心。

**Crispus:** Receive my blessings, young Hyacinthia. May you gain wings to return to the sky via the bridge of rainbows.

> **克里斯普斯：**接下我的赐福吧，年轻的雅辛忒丝。愿你能获得羽翼，搭乘彩虹之桥重返天空。

**Hyacine:** Thank you for your trust, Lord Crispus. We won't let you down...

> **风堇：**感谢您的信任，克里斯普斯阁下。我们一定不会辜负期望……

**Dan Heng:** ...Well done, Hyacine.

> **丹恒：**…做得好，风堇。

**Hyacine:** Heh... Thank you.

> **风堇：**嘿…谢谢夸奖。

**Hyacine:** My ancestors were all pretty strong-willed people. It won't be easy convincing them. I've run through these upcoming conversations in my head more times than I can count.

> **风堇：**我的先祖们都是些强直的人物，想要说服他们并不容易。同他们见面的一幕幕，我已经在心中演练过无数次啦。

**Dan Heng:** Looks like your efforts paid off.

> **丹恒：**看来你的努力没有白费。

- ▸ Witness Hyacine collect blessings from afar.

> ▸ 从远处见证风堇收集赐福。

Hyacine used the Chroma Coffer to summon the heroic spirit of the ancestor...

After a discussion, she obtained the first sky blessing from Magistrate Crispus.

**Hyacine:** Now, the next ancestor on the list... her spirit is said to rest in the Vortex of Genesis. A pity that the ceremony has to be performed in strict order. Shall we head back to Okhema together, Dannie?

> **风堇：**下一位要去拜访的先祖…据说她的英魂留在了创世涡心。可惜仪式必须按顺序进行，要搭伙回奥赫玛吗，丹宝？

**Dan Heng:** The Vortex of Genesis, huh...

> **丹恒：**创世涡心么……

**Dan Heng:** I have a suggestion.

> **丹恒：**那我倒是有个提议。

**Hyacine:** Mmm...?

> **风堇：**嗯…？

**Dan Heng:** Remember how you suggested I learn the "secret arts of water" from Phagousa's priests?

> **丹恒：**还记得么？一段时间以前，你推荐我学习法吉娜祭司们的「灵水秘术」……

**Dan Heng:** I've been practicing that in my spare time and made some progress. Now, as long as I have spirit water with me, I can use those techniques to carve a path directly to the Vortex of Genesis.

> **丹恒：**我用闲暇时间研习，取得了一些阶段性的成果。现在，只要随身携带「灵水」，我就能用秘术开拓出通向创世涡心的通路。

**Hyacine:** Ah, I remember! I didn't expect you to keep at it this long — that's so like you, Dannie.

> **风堇：**啊，我还记得！没想到你会一直坚持到现在，真不愧是丹宝。

**Dan Heng:** Compared to the currents of my home, the waters of Amphoreus are... rather untamed. Subduing the waters wasn't easy, but I've gotten the hang of it now.

> **丹恒：**和我故乡的洋流相比，翁法罗斯的水体要更…不羁。驯服它的过程有些艰难，还好我已大概摸清了其中的门路。

**Hyacine:** That makes sense. Phagousa was always described as a fickle Titan in the myths.

> **风堇：**毕竟在神话里，法吉娜本就是一位阴晴不定的泰坦嘛。

**Hyacine:** Since you're offering... then let me put your skills to the test, Dannie.

> **风堇：**既然丹宝都这么建议了…那我就来验收下你的学习成果吧？

**Dan Heng:** The spirit water gate formed with this technique isn't as stable as a ritual basin. It'll likely only let one person through.

> **丹恒：**用秘术临时创造的灵水之门远不如祭仪水盆那般稳定，恐怕只能允许一人通过。

**Dan Heng:** And as Phagousa's priests designed it — "the entrance serves as the exit." You'll need to come back through the vortex once you've met your ancestor's spirit. Little Ica and I will be here waiting.

> **丹恒：**以及，根据法吉娜祭司们的设计——「入口亦为出口」。待你见完先祖的灵魂，就通过涡心回到这里吧——我和小伊卡会在原地等你。

**Little Ica:** Doot... Doot-Doot!

> **小伊卡：**嘟…嘟嘟！

**Hyacine:** Got it. I'm counting on you, Dannie!

> **风堇：**我明白啦。那就拜托你了，丹宝！

**Dan Heng:** "...Under the raucous steps of their frenzied dance, the roar of celebration hides the world's heartbeat."

> **丹恒：**「…于它狂欢的舞步之下，以盛会的喧嚣掩盖世界的心跳。」

**Dan Heng:** "Spirit water, reveal the way —"

> **丹恒：**「灵水，揭露门扉吧——」

### Head to the Vortex of Genesis

**Hyacine:** The Vortex of Genesis... unbelievable. Dannie is full of surprises.

> **风堇：**创世涡心…真是不可思议，丹宝果真深藏不露呢。

**Hyacine:** But... This does feel way more disorienting than the ritual basin...

> **风堇：**就是…和祭仪水盆相比…眩晕感确实有些强烈。

**Hyacine:** Wait, is Lady Tribbie here too? And who is that next to her...?

> **风堇：**缇宝老师也在？在她身边那位……

**Hyacine:** Ah. I think I know who that is.

> **风堇：**啊——我大概能猜到那是谁啦。

**Tribbie:** ...Do you have to go already, Ciphy?

> **缇宝：**…飞儿，你这就要急着赶路了吗？

**Tribbie:** It's been ages since you were last in Okhema. You could've taken some time to rest, and we could've caught up...

> **缇宝：**好不容易回来奥赫玛一趟，你本可以好好休息一阵的，我们一起叙叙旧……

**Cipher:** That's alright, big sister Tribbie! I've gotten used to life on the road. Staying in this bustling city actually feels off.

> **赛飞儿：**不用了，缇宝阿姐！我早就过惯了四处漂泊的日子，让我在这熙熙攘攘的大城市留守反倒有些不自在。

**Cipher:** Besides, your prized student... gave me an incredibly tough mission to handle.

> **赛飞儿：**何况，你那位有出息的学生…可是给我派了个超级艰难的任务哪。

**Tribbie:** Cut Snowy some slack, Ciphy. He's still so young, yet he's shouldering all the responsibilities Agy left behind...

> **缇宝：**你要体谅一下小白呀，飞儿。他还这么年轻，就已经把阿雅留下来的的职责全都扛在了肩上……

**Tribbie:** Besides, Snowy's not the kind to ask for help lightly. Since he came to you, it means you're the best person for the job.

> **缇宝：**而且，小白可不是那种喜欢随便使唤别人的孩子。他会找到你，就说明这件事没有更合适的人选啦。

**Cipher:** You really spoil him, big sis Tribbie. Guess I'm past the age for someone to look after me...

> **赛飞儿：**你还真是宠溺他啊，缇宝阿姐。唉，可惜我已经过了那个处处需要被护短的年纪咯……

**Tribbie:** Ciphy —

> **缇宝：**飞儿——

**Cipher:** ...Just kidding, I get it. That Deliverer boy does have a certain charisma about him. It reminds me of Aglaea in her prime.

> **赛飞儿：**…开个玩笑哈，我懂你的意思。那救世小子身上的确有种让人信服的气质，不输当年的阿格莱雅。

**Cipher:** Anyway, I'm about to head out. Oh, big sis Tribbie, before I go — Got a little gift for you.

> **赛飞儿：**我这就准备出发了。啊，缇宝阿姐，临走前，我还有个礼物要送给你——

**Tribbie:** Wow, didn't expect you to be so thoughtful... What's in it?

> **缇宝：**哇，没想到你还这么有心…这里面装着什么呀？

**Cipher:** Heh... That's a part of the game too. If you want to see the full picture of what's inside, you'll have to wait until nightfall.

> **赛飞儿：**嘿嘿…这也是游戏的一环哪。想看到这件礼物的完整面貌，就等到天黑以后再打开它吧。

**Tribbie:** A gift with a mysterious twist? Seriously?

> **缇宝：**送人礼物怎么还带猜谜呀，真是的……

**Cipher:** ...Hmm? Looks like we have company.

> **赛飞儿：**…嗯？看来咱们有搭子了哪。

**Hyacine:** Lady Tribbie, and... this graceful lady must be Miss Cipher.

> **风堇：**缇宝老师，还有…这位身形轻盈的女士，一定就是赛飞儿小姐吧？

**Hyacine:** I'm Hyacinthia from the Twilight Courtyard. Everyone calls me Hyacine — pleased to meet you!

> **风堇：**我是昏光庭院的雅辛忒丝，大家叫我风堇——很高兴认识您！

**Cipher:** Tsk, bright and cheery as a lamp...

> **赛飞儿：**啧，这自带的明亮氛围……

**Cipher:** ...Ahem. Well, hello there, pink child. I'm Cifera from the long-fallen city-state of Dolos. As for the rest... it's all in the history books, so I don't need to repeat it here.

> **赛飞儿：**…咳，你好，粉彤彤的后辈。我是赛法利娅，来自早就陨落的城邦多洛斯。其它的…史书传记里都写了，也用不着我再介绍了吧？

**Cipher:** So you're from the Twilight Courtyard? Does that mean...

> **赛飞儿：**你说自己来自昏光庭院？也就是说……

**Hyacine:** Yes, I'm a descendant of the Sky people, the Chrysos Heir about to take over the authority of Sky.

> **风堇：**您没想错。我是天空一族的后嗣，将要接过「天空」权柄的黄金裔。

**Cipher:** Well, that's... unexpected. I figured Seliose's descendant would be... I don't know. Taller? Stronger?

> **赛飞儿：**那还真是…人不可貌相。我还以为塞涅俄丝的后裔会更…呃…高大威猛一点儿。

**Tribbie:** Don't judge a book by its cover, Ciphy. Cinny is a master of her craft. She's the most skilled and dependable physician in all of Amphoreus.

> **缇宝：**不能以貌取人呀，飞儿。小风堇在她的领域可是很厉害的专家哦，整个翁法罗斯都没有比她更靠谱的医师啦。

**Hyacine:** Don't worry, Lady Cipher. I may not be as strong and warlike as Lady Seliose from a thousand years ago, but I'm confident I can carry out the mission of the Skyfolk.

> **风堇：**请放心吧，赛飞儿女士。虽然我不像千年前的塞涅俄丝大人那样骁勇善战，但我有信心能完成天空后裔的使命。

**Cipher:** Ahaha, all this talk about missions... you're making it sound way too serious.

> **赛飞儿：**哎呀哈，什么使命不使命的，说得那么严肃……

**Cipher:** You've gotta look out for yourself, girl! Don't push yourself too hard. I'd hate to see that cute face all scratched up by Aquila's claws...

> **赛飞儿：**小姑娘，要学会优先保护好自己，别什么事都太拼命哈。瞧这张脸蛋，还挺可爱的，别叫艾格勒的爪子挠花了……

**Tribbie:** Ciphy, cracking jokes? Looks like you haven't forgotten what Agy said all those years ago.

> **缇宝：**飞儿现在也会这样逗人啦？看来你还惦念着阿雅很久以前的话呢。

**Cipher:** ...Anyway, I've got a mission to handle, so I'll get going.

> **赛飞儿：**…唔，我还有任务在身，先走一步了。

**Cipher:** Good luck, Miss Hyacine. And if things get tough, just let that Deliverer take the lead!

> **赛飞儿：**祝你成功，风堇姑娘——遇到什么难事，让那个救世小子顶在前面就行！

Cipher uses the Coin of Whimsy's ability and teleports away from the Vortex...

**Hyacine:** Miss Cipher is in such a hurry.

> **风堇：**赛飞儿小姐，真是个急性子呀。

**Tribbie:** Don't worry. She's always been like that... It's good to have her back in Okhema lending a hand.

> **缇宝：**她一直都是那样啦，别在意。不管怎样，飞儿她愿意回来奥赫玛帮忙…实在是太好了。

**Tribbie:** Well, enough about her for now. You're here because of your mission, aren't you, Cinny?

> **缇宝：**先不说她的事啦。小风堇会来到这里，是因为你身负的使命吧？

**Tribbie:** It's the perfect opportunity for me to go and tuck Ciphy's gift away somewhere safe. The Vortex of Genesis is all yours.

> **缇宝：**正好，我要去把飞儿送的礼物好好收起来——创世涡心就先让给你啦。

### Search for Ancestral Ghost

**Hyacine:** ...I will join the heroes of the prophecy to return to the sky and retrieve the Coreflame.

> **风堇：**…我将和神谕中的英雄们一道重返天空，取回火种。

**Hyacine:** Ancestor, please bestow upon me your generous blessing...

> **风堇：**先祖啊，请给予您慷慨的赐福吧……

- ▸ Speak with the ancestor.

> ▸ 与先祖交谈。

**Ancestral Ghost:** ...

> **先祖之灵：**……

**Ancestral Ghost:** ...Child, what brings you before me? Could it be... you're a descendant of the Sky people?

> **先祖之灵：**…孩子，为何你会出现在我面前？莫非…你是天空一族的后裔？

**Hyacine:** Yes. I am Hyacinthia of the Twilight Courtyard, the successor of the Sky people's Flame-Chase duty.

> **风堇：**您没猜错，我是昏光庭院的雅辛忒丝，天空一族逐火使命的继承者。

**Hyacine:** You must be General Ektra. Legend has it that during the Chrysos War, you led our people to ally with Okhema and held back the Kremnoan forces. It was your victory that earned the Sky people a place in the holy city.

> **风堇：**您应该是特克菈将军吧？传说在黄金战争中，您曾率领族人与奥赫玛结盟，抵御悬锋军队的进攻——您的战果为天空一族在圣城赢得了一席之地。

**General Ektra:** Hyacinthia... You seem well-versed in our clan's history.

> **特克菈将军：**雅辛忒丝么…看来你对我族的族谱十分熟悉啊，孩子。

**General Ektra:** And this is... the Vortex of Genesis?

> **特克菈将军：**这里是…创世涡心？

**Hyacine:** It is. Look — ten stars have already been lit. The Chrysos Heirs' Flame-Chase Journey is nearing its end.

> **风堇：**对。您看——有十颗星辰已经被点亮，黄金裔的逐火之旅已经临近完成。

**General Ektra:** Are those... the Coreflames of Phagousa and Georios?

> **特克菈将军：**那是…法吉娜和吉奥里亚的火种？

**General Ektra:** Does that mean Hysilens and the Mountain Dweller successfully carried out their duty?

> **特克菈将军：**那就是说，海瑟音和那位山之民…他们成功履行了自己的使命么？

**General Ektra:** Heh... As fellow pillars of Amphoreus, who would've thought that Sky's duty could only be fulfilled after a thousand years?

> **特克菈将军：**呵…同为翁法罗斯的支柱，没想到「天空」的宿命要等到千年之后才能完满啊。

**Hyacine:** It's because Sky shelters all beneath it. If it were to shatter, the world would fall apart with it. That's why Lady Aglaea made the Coreflame of Aquila the final step in our campaign.

> **风堇：**因为「天空」笼庇万物，它若分崩离析，世界也将被碾碎。所以，阿格莱雅大人才会将艾格勒的火种作为征途的收尾。

**General Ektra:** Aglaea...? Is she still on the Flame-Chase Journey after a thousand years?

> **特克菈将军：**阿格莱雅…？千年以后？她竟还走在逐火的道路上么……

**Hyacine:** ...Unfortunately, she has recently left us. But you're right — she was indeed still guiding the Chrysos Heirs forward.

> **风堇：**…很遗憾，她最近才离开了我们。但您说对了一部分…她的确仍在指引着黄金裔们前行。

**General Ektra:** Incredible. That girl actually managed to endure the merciless erosion of time... and lasted for so long.

> **特克菈将军：**不可思议。那姑娘竟能耐受住时光无情的磨蚀…一直坚持到这个年代。

**General Ektra:** Answer me, Hyacinthia. Since you recognize me, you must have also read my writings, no?

> **特克菈将军：**我问你，雅辛忒丝——既然你能认出我的身份，那你应当也读过我的亲笔？

**Hyacine:** Every record regarding my lineage is etched in my memory, including your words.

> **风堇：**和我族相关的史料，我全都熟记在心——当然也包括您留下的亲笔。

**General Ektra:** In that case, you're probably aware of the prophecy I sought from a certain Tribios.

> **特克菈将军：**那么，你应该知道，我曾从某位缇里西庇俄丝那里求得过一则预言。

**Hyacine:** Yes. "At the end of the rainbow bridge, the Skyfolk will mend dusk and dawn."

> **风堇：**当然——「在彩虹桥的尽头，天空之子将缝补晨昏。」

**Hyacine:** But unfortunately, no ancestor has ever been able to truly decipher its meaning.

> **风堇：**不过…很遗憾，至今还没有哪位先祖能解读出这道预言的涵义。

**General Ektra:** I see. Yet Tribios' prophecies never fail...

> **特克菈将军：**原来如此。但缇里西庇俄丝的预言从未落空……

**General Ektra:** Perhaps you'll be the one to witness its fulfillment, child. May the prophecy lead you to a gentle fate.

> **特克菈将军：**或许你会见证它应验，孩子。希望预言能为你带去温柔的命运。

**Hyacine:** Thank you, Lady Ektra. Whatever fate the prophecy holds, I'll face it head-on. It's only by giving everything we have that we can truly accept any outcome with a smile... won't you agree?

> **风堇：**谢谢您，特克菈大人。无论预言指向了何种结局，我都会迎头赶上——因为只有尽到了最大的努力，我们才能微笑着面对任何结果…您说对吗？

**General Ektra:** Hah... I like you, child. I wish this conversation could go on a little longer.

> **特克菈将军：**哈…我喜欢你，孩子。我多希望这场对话能再多延续一阵。

**General Ektra:** Take my blessing, Hyacinthia. When I first saw you, I indeed had my doubts...

> **特克菈将军：**收下我的祝福吧，雅辛忒丝。初见你时，我心中确有担忧……

**General Ektra:** But your resolve has touched me. If Sky's divinity is fated to pass on... I hope it incorporates your optimism and kindness.

> **特克菈将军：**…但你的信念打动了我。如果天空的神性注定要被取代…我希望它能接过你的乐观和温柔。

**Hyacine:** Your blessing is a treasure beyond measure, General. Rest well... I'll make sure your gift is put to its best use.

> **风堇：**您的认可是无价之宝，将军。请安歇吧…我一定会将您的馈赠物尽其用。

> **风堇：**就是这里，我们到啦。

> **风堇：**但光荣的决斗背后…是悲壮的落幕。祭典结束不久，祖母的伤情恶化，最终逝于异乡…这座算碑就是悬锋人应她的遗愿留下的最后纪念。

**Hyacine:** Her death marked the final collapse of the Twilight Courtyard, already fragile under the shadow of war.

> **风堇：**而祖母的去世，也使得战争阴影下本就摇摇欲坠的昏光庭院最终解体。

**Dan Heng:** And the Kremnoan warrior she fought against...

> **丹恒：**那位和她展开死斗的悬锋勇士……

**Hyacine:** ...was Lord Krateros.

> **风堇：**…是克拉特鲁斯阁下。

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

> **丹恒：**但你还是救下了他的性命，尽管他夺走了你的至亲。

**Hyacine:** Seeing him lying there, gravely wounded, bleeding out... I can't put what I felt at that moment into words.

> **风堇：**看到身负重伤的他倒在地上，血流不止…我很难用语言表达那时的复杂心绪。

**Hyacine:** But when I heard Lord Krateros took that fatal blow to protect Lady Trianne... All those tangled feelings faded away.

> **风堇：**但当我听说，克拉特鲁斯阁下是为了保护缇安老师才挨下了沉痛的一击，我内心的杂念便消散了。

**Hyacine:** To quote Lord Phainon, Krateros must have done that "to become a better man."

> **风堇：**用白厄阁下的话说，他一定是成为了「更好的人」。

**Dan Heng:** It takes true courage to forgive. You're every bit as strong as your ancestors, Hyacine.

> **丹恒：**只有足够强大的心灵才能学会宽恕。你并不逊于你的先祖们，风堇。

**Hyacine:** Heh... You're getting better at this, Dannie.

> **风堇：**嘿…你越来越会聊天啦，丹宝。

**Hyacine:** In her final diary entry, my grandmother wrote that when the moment of destiny comes, a descendant of the Sky people would deliver the message of their departure to her spirit.

> **风堇：**祖母在最后的日记中说，她希望当使命召唤之时，天空一族的后人能将启程的消息带给她留在世间的魂灵。

**Hyacine:** I wonder what she'd think... When the one awaking her turns out to be her beloved granddaughter.

> **风堇：**当她发现唤醒自己的竟是她曾宠爱的孙女…不知道祖母会作何反应呢？

**Hyacine:** ...I will join the heroes of the prophecy to return to the sky and retrieve the Coreflame.

> **风堇：**…我将和神谕中的英雄们一道重返天空，取回火种。

**Hyacine:** Ancestor, please bestow upon me your generous blessing...

> **风堇：**先祖啊，请给予您慷慨的赐福吧……

- ▸ Listen carefully to Hyacine talk with her grandmother.

> ▸ 仔细聆听风堇与祖母的交谈。

**Jacyntha:** ...

> **雅辛缇娅：**……

**Jacyntha:** Is this... the world of the living?

> **雅辛缇娅：**这是…生者的世界？

**Jacyntha:** Wait... You... could you be...

> **雅辛缇娅：**等等，你，难道是……

**Hyacine:** Grandmother, it's me, your Hyacinthia.

> **风堇：**祖母——是我，您的雅辛忒丝。

**Jacyntha:** Hyacinthia, my little hummingbird! Why... how... Why is it you who awakened me???

> **雅辛缇娅：**雅辛忒丝，我的小蜂鸟！为何是你…将我唤醒的怎会是你？！

**Jacyntha:** I have never imagined this... I thought I would feel happy for all my ancestors when this day came, but now...

> **雅辛缇娅：**我从未想过这种情景…我本以为这一天来临时，我会为所有先祖感到释然。但现在……

**Jacyntha:** I can't even experience the joy of seeing you again!

> **雅辛缇娅：**现在，我甚至无法尽享这与你重逢的喜悦！

**Hyacine:** Do you remember that... you and Father took me to my very first Sky Rite when I was eight?

> **风堇：**您还记得…在我刚满八周岁时，您和父亲带我参加人生中第一次天空祭礼时的情境吗？

**Hyacine:** Back then, you wanted me to pick one accessory out of four: The sun crown representing nobility, the storm bracelet symbolizing strength, the ice ring embodying wisdom, and the radiant pendant signifying kindness...

> **风堇：**那时，您让我从四件配饰中选择一件：代表尊贵的烈阳头环，代表力量的雷雨手镯，象征着智慧的冰雪戒指，还有喻义友善的微光吊坠……

**Hyacine:** Grandmother, do you remember the one I chose?

> **风堇：**祖母，您还记得我那时的选择吗？

**Jacyntha:** You spoke an innocent little prayer, infusing a faint thread of light to that modest pendant...

> **雅辛缇娅：**那时…你吟诵着稚嫩的祷词，为那枚不起眼的吊坠引来了一抹微光……

**Hyacine:** Yes, and I remember how surprised you were. In all those centuries, no other child of the Sky people had ever made the same choice I did.

> **风堇：**对。而且，您的反应还有些吃惊——因为在过去的几百年间，没有哪个天空一族的孩子跟我做了同样的选择。

**Jacyntha:** Hyacinthia... There was a time when I thought you were a timid child.

> **雅辛缇娅：**雅辛忒丝…我一度以为你是个怯懦的孩子。

**Jacyntha:** You never had much interest in swords or battle, and had no relation to that fiery determination and strength in our blood.

> **雅辛缇娅：**你对兵戈武技兴致寥寥，性格中也全然不见我们一族中的刚强跟硬朗。

**Jacyntha:** Yet I've told myself — that's not a bad thing. If my hummingbird could live her life in kindness and optimism, then it'd be a good thing.

> **雅辛缇娅：**但我始终告诉自己：那没什么不好。如果我的小蜂鸟能在乐观和善意中度过一生，那绝非她的不幸。

**Jacyntha:** I never thought the burden of retrieving the Coreflame of Aquila would fall to you...

> **雅辛缇娅：**我未曾想过，取回艾格勒火种的重担果真会落在你的身上……

**Hyacine:** Don't worry about me, Grandmother. I believe what makes someone a Skyfolk isn't just outstanding strength, but the capacity to shape the world.

> **风堇：**请别替我担心，祖母。在我看来，定义天空之子血脉的不只是杰出的力量，还有我们捏塑世界的天赋。

**Hyacine:** When the world cried out for a hero to challenge the gods a thousand years ago, Seliose stepped forward and began the Flame-Chasing era...

> **风堇：**千年前，当世界急需一位敢于挑战神明的英雄时，英雄塞涅俄丝站了出来，开启了逐火的时代……

**Hyacine:** ...And now, as the Flame-Chase Journey nears its end, this broken world is crying out for something else — healing.

> **风堇：**…而在逐火之旅即将步入尾声的这一刻，眼前满目疮痍的世界正急切地呼求着疗愈。

**Hyacine:** Isn't it poetic? A saga that spans a thousand years began with Sky, and will also end with Sky...

> **风堇：**这是多么美妙的巧合呀——千年的英雄史诗，以「天空」作为开篇，亦由「天空」写下结语……

**Jacyntha:** Heh... you're still as optimistic as ever, just like when you were a kid.

> **雅辛缇娅：**呵…你还是那么乐观啊，和小时候一模一样。

**Jacyntha:** If your mind is set, Hyacinthia, then carry your resolve through to the very end.

> **雅辛缇娅：**既然你已经下定决心，那就将你的信念贯彻到底吧，雅辛忒丝。

**Jacyntha:** Return to the sky with my blessing, and watch over this world on the threshold of salvation.

> **雅辛缇娅：**带着我的赐福回归天空，俯瞰这个行将接受拯救的世界吧。

**Hyacine:** Grandmother... thank you, but I have one last request.

> **风堇：**祖母…谢谢您。但我还剩最后一个请求。

**Jacyntha:** Speak, Hyacinthia. We should still have some time.

> **雅辛缇娅：**说吧，雅辛忒丝。我们应该还有时间。

**Hyacine:** Before you go... could you tell me the tale of the sky hero once more, just like you used to?

> **风堇：**在您离开以前，可否请您像当年那样…再为我讲述一次天空英雄的传说？

**Jacyntha:** Hehe...

> **雅辛缇娅：**呵……

**Jacyntha:** Then, listen closely, my dearest hummingbird. After all, this will be the last time...

> **雅辛缇娅：**那你一定要仔细聆听，我最亲爱的小蜂鸟。毕竟，这就是最后一次了……

**Jacyntha:** For millennia, the Titans waged relentless wars, leaving the world scarred and ravaged.

> **雅辛缇娅：**泰坦征伐千载，世界满目疮痍。

**Jacyntha:** A century of the Chrysos War plunged the nations into chaos and decay.

> **雅辛缇娅：**黄金战争百年，诸邦混乱衰疲。

**Jacyntha:** Until a spear, fueled by mortal rage...

> **雅辛缇娅：**直到，一支奔腾着凡人之怒的长枪……

**Jacyntha:** Pierced through the smoke and flames that buried the prophecy of deliverance.

> **雅辛缇娅：**刺破那掩埋了救世神谕的烟尘与战火。

**Jacyntha:** Aquila, the celestial shaper, the hundred-eyed divine bird crowned with the blazing sun and wielding thunder...

> **雅辛缇娅：**捏塑天象的艾格勒，手握惊雷、额戴赫日的百目神鸟……

**Jacyntha:** Though struck blind in ninety-nine eyes by Nikador, they kept one eye, glaring at the children of twilight who received divine blessing yet turned their weapons against the gods.

> **雅辛缇娅：**虽为尼卡多利戳盲九十九目，仍独留一只眼睛，怒视那领受它赐福、却举戈相向的晨昏后嗣。

**Jacyntha:** The Chrysos Heir of the Sky, Seliose the Daythunder Knight, ascended the forbidden lands of the Eye of Twilight at dusk, clad in wings.

> **雅辛缇娅：**天空的黄金裔，「阳雷骑士」塞涅俄丝，在黄昏时分，身披羽翼、登临晨昏之眼的禁土。

**Jacyntha:** To her left was the lion-headed Solabis, the Sunlit Wings, whose roars breathed divine flames and scorched the world.

> **雅辛缇娅：**她的左侧是狮首的「烈阳之翼」索拉比斯，咆哮中吐纳灼世的神火；

**Jacyntha:** To her right was the horse-bodied Lunabis, the Moonlit Plume, whose graceful steps trampled the stars in the heavens.

> **雅辛缇娅：**她的右侧是马身的「星月之翎」露奈比斯，旋步中踢踏漫天的星罗。

**Jacyntha:** The one warrior and her two beasts challenged the ruler of the sky, determined to seize the divine Coreflame through plunder.

> **雅辛缇娅：**一人两兽，向天空的主宰发起挑战，誓将弑夺神明的火种。

**Jacyntha:** The Titan's every roar summoned lightning bolts, and their every movement stirred cyclones.

> **雅辛缇娅：**泰坦的每声怒吼都横生落雷，泰坦的每次倾身都卷起飓风。

**Jacyntha:** The wounded Titan's blood... poured down like boiling rain upon the world and converged into scalding torrents.

> **雅辛缇娅：**泰坦受伤的血液…在世间坠下沸腾的血雨，汇成滚烫的洪流。

**Jacyntha:** In the final moments of the battle, the Sunlit Wings set the Titan's wings aflame, while the Moonlit Plume sealed the divine body...

> **雅辛缇娅：**战斗的最后，「烈阳之翼」点燃了泰坦的羽翼，「星月之翎」封锁了泰坦的神躯……

**Jacyntha:** The battered hero of the sky, with her wings broken and her body covered in wounds, struggled to lift her spear... and pierced the last monstrous eye.

> **雅辛缇娅：**那翅翼折断、伤痕累累的天空英雄，艰难抬起长枪…将那最后一只恫世的巨眼穿透。

**Jacyntha:** The Titan's divine body crashed to the ground, and with it, the sky tore apart like fabric, about to fall with its god.

> **雅辛缇娅：**泰坦的神躯轰然倒下…与此同时，苍穹撕挦如同裂帛，天空即将与神明一同陨落。

**Jacyntha:** The fear and pleas of mortals urged the hero of the sky to rise again.

> **雅辛缇娅：**凡人的惊惶与祈求，令天空英雄再度起身。

**Jacyntha:** Seliose the Daythunder Knight, the first of the Chrysos Heirs to slay a god in this world, charged into the crumbling body of Aquila with her two fearless winged companions.

> **雅辛缇娅：**「阳雷骑士」塞涅俄丝，世上首度弑神的黄金裔，与两位骁勇的翼兽伙伴，径直冲入艾格勒逐渐溃散的躯干。

**Jacyntha:** She merged with the Titan, holding up the sky once more, only to leave behind a powerful message for the world —

> **雅辛缇娅：**她与泰坦融为一体，再度撑起天穹，只为世间留下一句激昂的寄语——

**Jacyntha:** "My descendants will be blessed and cross the rainbow bridge back to the sky... At the end of the Prophecy of Genesis, they will reclaim the Coreflame from my hands."

> **雅辛缇娅：**「我的后裔终将领受祝福，脚踏虹桥重登天空…在那创世神谕的终点，从我手中带回这枚火种。」

- ▸ Witness Hyacine collect blessings from afar.

> ▸ 从远处见证风堇收集赐福。

Hyacine used the Chroma Coffer to summon the heroic spirit of the ancestor...

After a discussion, Hyacine obtained the third sky blessing from her grandmother, Jacyntha.

At the moment of parting, Grandmother told Hyacine one last time the legend of the hero of the sky — Seliose the Daythunder Knight...

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 132　Poet, Speak of the Sky Through Me (I)　诗者啊，从我处诉说天空·上

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Demigod Council" Dawncloud　|　角色：Trailblazer; Mem; Phainon; Mnemosyne; Hyacine; Dan Heng; Lunabis; Solabis; Little Ica; Caenis; Seliose　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Grove, Judge the Past and Present'' completed

### Head to the "Demigod Council" Dawncloud and continue your adventure

> *The Eye of Twilight, a sky barrier named after a Titan, was both the homeland of the Sky people in ancient times and now serves as Aquila's dwelling. With Hyacine's help, we have set foot on this outworld land to find the Titan who binds the sky.*

A few days later, at Dawncloud...

**(Trailblazer):** (Today's the day...)

> **开拓者：**（就在今天了……）

**Mem:** Wow, I didn't expect it to be so lively up here...

> **迷迷：**哇，没想到这么高的地方，居然会这么热闹……

**Mem:** Are these people here to send off the Chrysos Heirs?

> **迷迷：**这里的人们，是来为黄金裔们送行的吗？

**(Trailblazer):** There's Phainon. Let's go talk to him.

> **开拓者：**白厄在那儿——去找他聊聊吧。

**Hyacine:** Please keep holding on to what's left of your will. And I will... reawaken you as a heroic soul.

> **风堇：**请牢牢抓住自己残存的意识，我会让你…以英魂的姿态再次苏醒。

**Solabis:** *roaring*

> **索拉比斯：** *低吼*

- ▸ I wonder how much it understood.

> **风堇：**一定能听懂的…虽然深陷黑潮的苦痛，但它是位智慧而坚强的生灵。

> **白厄：**索拉比斯，传说中陪伴在英雄身边的「烈阳之翼」，如果这场战斗能让你回想起当年的伟绩，那会是我的荣幸……

> **白厄：**我们已令其他泰坦一一安息。如今，逐火的终点正是这座天空堡垒的主人。

> **白厄：**请引领我们去往艾格勒面前吧，两位英魂。弑神取火的纪元由塞涅俄丝亲手开启，哪怕她无法亲眼看到一个时代的终结，至少你们能代为见证。

**Solabis:** Confidence and fierceness — Yes, I do indeed see a shadow of her in you.

> **索拉比斯：**我直面过天空的暴君，它的怒火足以熔化天穹——并非夸大其词，因为翁法罗斯的苍天即是它的权柄。

> **风堇：**为了遵守与英雄塞涅俄丝的约定，她的后裔用了一千年的时光守望天空。我想，那世世代代为同一个理想而活的先祖们，他们有权知道传说的真相。

**Hyacine:** I must figure out the truth behind the legend, even if it's for my ancestors' sake.

**Seliose the Daythunder Knight:** Those innocent children of the earth were sentenced to total annihilation by the god above, while they had nothing more than mere curiosity for things that lie beyond the sky...

**Seliose the Daythunder Knight:** Look at yourselves. You call yourselves the Sunborn, and yet you're unable to bear the blinding sunlight. You even consider sunburns and blisters as a gift from the gods.

**Seliose the Daythunder Knight:** You declare yourselves the chosen ones, champions favored by Aquila, yet you dare not even declare war upon the true threat of this world. Instead, you raise your swords and rattle them at your weaker brethren.

**Seliose the Daythunder Knight:** You are all beyond saving... My father was also a disciple of the Sun, but he taught me resilience and justice and guided me toward the light...

**Seliose the Daythunder Knight:** ...He taught me wherever there is light, there is hope. Wretched people, I see no hope left in any of you... Even a shadow has more dignity than you lot!

**Resentful Sunfolk:** Even so, Daughter of Dusk! Even so, you should not shatter our faith...

**Mourning Sunfolk:** ...Not just our faith! Aquila forged the sky — nay, they are the sky itself! If they fall, what will this world turn into!?

**Seliose the Daythunder Knight:** A prophecy echoes from the horizon's edge. It heralds the advent of a new age, one not ruled by the Titans, but created by mortal heroes.

**Seliose the Daythunder Knight:** I choose to believe in that prophecy! I must believe in any sign that promises to break the chains imposed on us by the old era... Any sign that will lead us to a new chapter of the world!

**Seliose the Daythunder Knight:** If you wish to stop me from slaying the god, you'd better muster your courage and draw your bows. Let's see if I will be pierced by a hail of arrows first... or if you will be burned to ash by Solabis!

**Despairing Sunfolk:** How can we even fight you? You're the powerful Daythunder Knight, a warrior goddess capable of trampling all lands...

**Seliose the Daythunder Knight:** I am not a god, but the only mortal in this cage who is brave enough to defy the warden.

The Sunfolks in the memory fall silent. Their expressions are ashen despite being under the bright sunlight.

**Seliose the Daythunder Knight:** See... You are all truly weak beyond measure.

**Seliose the Daythunder Knight:** The hunting ground beckons. Come, Solabis.

> **风堇：**天空的神明，我们来了…你的怨愤和惶恐很快就会结束。

**Hyacine:** Lady Seliose... do you still have your humanity after merging with the Titan? I hope you can witness the harvest of the seeds you once sowed.

> **风堇：**塞涅俄丝大人…与泰坦融为一体后，你还维持着人类的意识吗？我希望你能看到，自己种下的种子结出了怎样的果实。

**Hyacine:** Now... only the final steps remain.

> **风堇：**现在…就只剩下最后的几步了。

**Mem:** I can feel the memories here churning. It's as if countless emotions have lingered through the ages, refusing to fade away.

> **迷迷：**人家能清晰地感觉到，这里的记忆在翻涌。就好像…有许多感情在漫长的岁月中彷徨，不愿就此消失。

**Mem:** Like ghosts lingering by the River of Souls...

> **迷迷：**就像…在冥河边徘徊的魂灵……

> **白厄：**你准备好了么，风堇？

**Hyacine:** Yes. The Flame-Chase Journey is reaching its final chapter. No matter how cruel the truth may be, nothing will sway my determination to finish this mission.

> **风堇：**嗯。逐火之旅已然走到最后，无论所谓的历史真相如何残酷，任何事都不会动摇我完成使命的决心。

> **白厄：**但被尘封的历史真相也必须重见天日，因为凡人的命运不该被经过美化的传说淹没。

> **风堇：**我们的想法完全一致，白厄阁下。

**Solabis:** Go to the celestial globe, Hyacinthia.

> **索拉比斯：**去接触浑象仪吧，雅辛忒丝。

**Solabis:** The forgotten past will reveal itself to you, one fragment at a time. Hold fast to your resolve — you'll need it to face what lies ahead.

> **索拉比斯：**那些被世界遗忘的过往会在你眼前一一重现。牢牢抓住你那不可动摇的信念，你会需要它的支撑。

A thousand years ago, at Dawncloud...

**???:** To all citizens of Okhema — today, I bring you two pieces of news: one sorrowful, one joyous.

> **？？？：** *奥赫玛的全体公民哪——今天，我要给你们带来两个消息：一则悲报，一则佳音。*

**"Atticus?":** But citizens, do not grieve his passing! In his final moments, Chaplain Phoros found peace. While he still had his wits, he heard the final words of the sky father...

> **感恩的公民：**直到…永远？

**Cipher:**— The light of the Dawn Device never fades... Kephale's protection has no end!

> **赛飞儿：**高估了自己啊……

> **赛飞儿：**「哪怕到头来也只是个小偷…我……」

> **赛飞儿：**「求你了……」

**Cipher:** ...

> **赛飞儿：**……

**Aglaea:** You are the unsung hero of Amphoreus, Cifera. You saved us all.

> **阿格莱雅：**你是翁法罗斯湮没无闻的英雄，赛法利娅。你拯救了我们所有人。

> **阿格莱雅：**你出卖了世界……

**Cipher:** If there were a lie so brilliant that it could fool everyone...

A few days ago, in Okhema's "Twilight Courtyard"...

**Clementine:** Miss Hyacine... are you really leaving?

### Console the frightened residents

> *Shielded by Castorice's power, Mydei buys us time against the Flame Reaver, enabling our escape from Dawncloud to Marmoreal Palace. Though this place too lies in ruins, we're relieved to find Okhema's residents unharmed under Hyacine's protection. The Vortex of Genesis awaits just a short distance away — we must hasten our steps.*

**Phainon:** Everyone, you're all safe... for now.

**Phainon:** The first story taught me that the fate of a world should be in the hands of its people. And... even if the Nameless are willing to risk everything for an unfamiliar world, in the end you still have to embark on a new journey and go to the next world that needs saving.

> **白厄：**因为那就是你们的使命…你们踏上的「命途」。

**Phainon:** After hearing those stories, I made up my mind...
