**Phainon:** I won't let your journey end here, (Trailblazer). Somewhere beyond the sky, where Amphoreans have never reached, there are many people waiting for you to come home.

> **白厄：**我绝不会让你们的旅途在这里画上句号，开拓者。在翁法罗斯人从未触及的天外，还有很多人在等着你们回家。

**Phainon:** The prophecy has always remained shrouded in ambiguity — even if the twelfth Coreflame is returned, no one knows how the Era Nova will manifest... let alone if the miracle will extend its favor to two visitors from beyond the sky.

> **白厄：**神谕模糊不清，即便归还了第十二颗火种，也没人知道「再创世」究竟会以怎样的面目显现…更遑论「奇迹」是否能眷顾两名天外来客。

**Phainon:** Before stepping into a fate unknown, the last thing I can do... is help you return to your family.

> **白厄：**在踏入未知的命运之前，我能为你们做到的最后一件事…就是帮助你们回到家人的身边。

- ▸ And even if we ignore the prophecy, we can't go back now.

**Zeph the Guard:** Please don't worry, (Trailblazer). Lord Phainon has already entrusted Elder Lygus to fix your transport.

> **卫士泽佛：**请别担心，开拓者阁下。白厄大人此前已经委托了来古士议员，修好了两位的载具。

**Mnemosyne the Guard:** Lady Trinnon's Century Gate is open on the main street outside Okhema's walls, (Trailblazer).

> **卫士尼莫西妮：**缇宁大人的百界门就开在奥赫玛的城外大道，开拓者阁下。

**Phainon:** I'm sorry for making such an unreasonable request of you all. But we live in an imperfect age... where there is never a perfect choice.

> **白厄：**对不起，向你们提出如此过分的请求。但我们活在一个不完美的时代里…不存在两全其美的选择。

- ▸ I have the right to witness the ending of this Trailblaze.
- ▸ Don't try to stop me from coming back like that.
- ▸ A hero... doesn't have to bear everything alone.
- ▸ You've changed into someone different from yourself, Phainon.

**Phainon:** Every one of us has the right to make our own choices. If you can't accept my decision on your behalf...

> **白厄：**我们每个人都有抉择的权力。假如无法接受我代替你们做出的决定……

**Phainon:** ...Then use your choice to open up a new path — that's what you're best at, right?

> **白厄：**…那就用你自己的选择开辟出新的道路吧——这是你最擅长的事，对么？

**Phainon:** Regardless... Dan Heng is still holding on for his life... Don't keep him waiting too long, (Trailblazer).

> **白厄：**但无论如何…丹恒现在还在苦苦支撑。别让他等太久，开拓者。

**Phainon:** One day, I hope we will reunite under the constellations... When that time comes, we can hold a belated victory feast. How about that?

> **白厄：**希望有朝一日，我们能在星宿的见证下重聚…到时候，我们再摆一席迟到的庆功宴吧。

**Phainon:** The spirit water... has dried up?

> **白厄：**灵水…干涸了？

**Phainon:** Blast! Why is nothing going right...

> **白厄：**该死！为什么处处都是阻碍……

**Phainon:** Calm down... There might be another way in. The closest ritual basin, the other Chrysos Heirs'...

> **白厄：**冷静下来…也许还有别的入口。离这里最近的，其他黄金裔的祭仪水盆……

**Trinnon:** ...Snowy.

> **缇宁：**…小白。

**Phainon:** ...Trinnon!

> **白厄：**…缇宁老师！

**Phainon:** I thought you'd be waiting at the Vortex of Genesis. Are you all right?

> **白厄：**我以为你会在创世涡心等我。你还好吗？

**Trinnon:** When Okhema's night descended... all the spirit water dried up along with it. We anticipated that you'd be on edge, so we came ahead to wait for you.

> **缇宁：**奥赫玛的夜幕降临时…所有的灵水也都随之枯涸了。*我们*猜到你会着急，所以才提前来这里等着。

**Phainon:** Heh... You've always been the ones who knew me best.

> **白厄：**呵…你们一直是最了解我的人。

**Phainon:** Kephale's Coreflame... You know where it is?

> **白厄：**刻法勒的火种…你知道它在哪儿吗？

**Trinnon:** Yes. Little Ciphy protected the Coreflame well... No one would be able to guess where she stashed it.

> **缇宁：**嗯。小飞儿，她把火种保护得很好…没人能猜到它被藏在了哪里。

**Trinnon:** Bartholos! You can come on out!

> **缇宁：**巴特鲁斯——出来吧？

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** ...Heeheehee! I don't have to pretend to be an envelope anymore! That was suffocating!

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**…桀桀桀！总算不用装成个信封了，可憋死我了！

**Phainon:** Bartholos!? What in the world...

> **白厄：**巴特鲁斯？！这究竟是……

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Hey, you'd better watch your tone, brat! That Coreflame that the entire world is after...

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**喂喂，你现在态度可得放尊重点呀，小鬼！因为整个世界都在觊觎的那颗火种……

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** ...is currently nestled in the depths of my bottomless tummy!

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**…现在可就躺在本大爷深不见底的胃囊里呀！

**Phainon:** This certainly was... unexpected.

> **白厄：**这的确…在意料之外。

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Huh? Your reaction's too composed. It's completely different from what I was anticipating!

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**哈？你的反应未免太冷静了，这和我设想的完全不一样嘛！

**Trinnon:** Bartholos... Snowy still doesn't know the truth behind Okhema's eternal daylight.

> **缇宁：**巴特鲁斯…小白还不知道奥赫玛永昼的真相。

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Ah... you're right. Then you'd better strap in and listen up, brat!

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**啊…对哦。那你可要站稳听好咯，小鬼！

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** That girl, Cifera, spun a grand lie that deceived all of Amphoreus...

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**赛法利娅那丫头，用一个伟大的谎言骗过了整个翁法罗斯——

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** She tricked all of you into believing that that giant ball on Kephale's back would cast light over Okhema forever...

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**她骗过了你们所有人，让你们相信刻法勒背上那个大球会永远照亮奥赫玛……

**"Spirithief" Bartholos:** Not just that... Heeheehee, she even managed to fool that dumb ascension trial, and allowed me, the great Zagreus... to survive!

> **「贼灵」巴特鲁斯：**除此之外…桀桀桀，她还骗过了那个愚蠢的晋升试炼，让本大爷，伟大的扎格列斯…活了下来！

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** ...Hey, brat?

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**…喂？小鬼？

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** You... still no reaction from you? Dang it! You're no fun at all. I'm sorely disappointed!

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**你…怎么还是没反应呀？可恶！一点都不有趣，本大爷太失望了！

**Phainon:** So, for the last thousand years... what maintained Okhema's eternal daylight was not Kephale's miracle, but Cipher's lie woven using the divine authority of Trickery...

> **白厄：**所以，千年以来…维系奥赫玛永昼的并非刻法勒的神迹，而是赛飞儿以「诡计」的神权编织的谎言……

**Phainon:** But now, the lie has come undone. Which means...

> **白厄：**但现在，谎言破碎了。那就代表……

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** You didn't guess wrong... Cifera has already passed, and all her lies have scattered to the winds like ash.

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**你没猜错…赛法利娅已经逝去了。她所有的谎言，如今也都灰飞云散了啊。

**Phainon:** But you just said, you — Zagreus — relied on Cipher's lie to keep living. If she is already...

> **白厄：**但你刚刚才说过，你——扎格列斯——同样是靠着赛飞儿的谎言维系生命的。假如她已经……

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** Heeheehee... Guess you're not as smart as I thought, brat. Don't forget. I am one of the Twelve Titans of Amphoreus...

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**桀桀桀…看来你的机灵程度也有限呀，小鬼。别忘了，本大爷可是翁法罗斯的十二泰坦之一……

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** And this Coreflame in my belly... Is it not the core that maintains a Titan's life and divinity?

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**而我胃囊里的这颗火种…不正是用来维系泰坦生命和神性的核心嘛？

**Phainon:** Ah...

> **白厄：**啊……

**Phainon:** So, you're... going to hand the Coreflame over to us, just like that?

> **白厄：**所以，你…打算就这么把火种交给我们？

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** Heeheehee... Just so! How about it? Let's hear some groveling gratitude, eh?

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**桀桀桀…正是如此！怎么样，还不快谢过本大爷？

**Phainon:** Why? In the myths... Zagreus was the most cowardly and shameful of the Titans.

> **白厄：**为什么？神话中…扎格列斯是最胆小偷生的泰坦。

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** Hey, stop slandering me with those fabricated tales! My pursuits are far greater than one measly life...

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**哎，少拿那些编纂出来的故事诋毁本大爷！我的追求，可比区区一条小命远大得多……

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** Let me tell you then! My lifelong ambition... was to work with Cipher to conjure the greatest trick in the world!

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**告诉你吧！本泰坦毕生所求…就是跟赛飞儿一起，编织出这世上最伟大的诡计！

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** Assume this world holds a hundred million possibilities, and no matter how one looks at it, ninety-nine million, nine hundred ninety-nine thousand, nine hundred and ninety-nine of them all point to outcomes of certain destruction...

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**假设这个世界的未来存在一亿种可能性，不管怎么看，那其中的九千九百九十九万九千九百九十九种都指向了毁灭的结局……

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** But! With an exquisitely brilliant scheme, we fooled all the fools, villains, and disasters of this world, and achieved that one-in-a-hundred-million possibility...

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**但是！凭着这一桩精妙的诡计，我们骗过了这世上所有的白痴、恶徒、灾难，实现了那亿分之一的可能……

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** This... is the "miracle" I wished to achieve in my life's pursuit, even at the cost of turning into this!

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**这…就是我穷极一生，不惜变成这副模样，也想实现的「神迹」呀！

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** Take the Coreflame with you, brat! Go finish your mission!

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**把火种拿去吧，小鬼！去完成你的使命！

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** Remember this well... What saved Amphoreus wasn't the grace of that simpleton Kephale, nor was it the babbling of that child Oronyx...

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**记好咯——拯救了翁法罗斯的，不是刻法勒那个呆子的恩泽，也不是欧洛尼斯那个娃娃的呓语……

**Zagreus, Embers of Trickery:** ...It was the divine power of me, scorned by all... and the millennium-long lie of a cat-eared thief!

> **扎格列斯，诡计的余烬：**…而是本大爷遭世人唾弃的神力…还有一个猫耳小贼维系了千年的谎言！

Zagreus takes out the Coreflame that belonged to Kephale, which was hidden in their stomach.

Their divine body, reduced to the form of a Spirithief, disappears into dust. Their unbridled laughter fades away in the wind...

**Phainon:** Strife. Death. Trickery...

> **白厄：**纷争。死亡。诡计……

**Phainon:** So those signs deemed as harbingers of calamity... will also leave behind marks in the tale of deliverance.

> **白厄：**原来那些被视为灾厄的符号…也能在救世的故事里留下印记。

**Trinnon:** With this, the final Coreflame can be returned to its rightful place.

> **缇宁：**如此一来，最后的一颗火种就能归位啦。

**Trinnon:** The spirit water in the holy city has all dried up... We can try to break the sealing magic of Phagousa's priests... and use the Century Gate to send you into the Vortex of Genesis.

> **缇宁：**圣城中的灵水已经干涸…不过，*我们*可以尽力突破法吉娜祭司的封印法术…用「百界门」把你送入创世涡心。

**Trinnon:** But... that will likely exhaust any remaining power of Janus that we have. After that, the rest is up to you, Snowy.

> **缇宁：**但…那恐怕会耗尽我们身上剩下的雅努斯神力。后面的事就都靠你啦，小白。

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Phainon:** Farewells... I've almost grown numb to them at this point. Right now... I can't even utter a plain goodbye.

> **白厄：**离别…我已经快要对它感到麻木了。到了这个时候…我连一句干涩的道别都说不出口。

**Trinnon:** Snowy...

> **缇宁：**小白……

**Phainon:** Don't worry, Trinnon. I'm no longer the empty shell that Aglaea brought back to Okhema.

> **白厄：**别担心，缇宁老师。我已经不再是被阿格莱雅带回奥赫玛的那具空壳了。

**Phainon:** For every companion who couldn't reach the end, their conviction to save the world became a part of me, piecing together the soul I once lost.

> **白厄：**每一位没能走到最后的同伴，他们救世的信念都会成为我的一部分，拼凑出我一度丢失的灵魂。

**Phainon:** I lost my home village, but you, Aglaea, and everyone else created a second home for me.

> **白厄：**我失去了故乡，但你、阿格莱雅、还有所有其他人，共同为我编织出了第二个家园。

**Phainon:** Now that this home is also in danger... it is my turn to repay your kindness and create a new world that can carry everyone's wishes.

> **白厄：**如今，这个家园也危在旦夕…那就该轮到我回报你们的馈赠，去创造一个能承载所有愿望的新世界了。

**Phainon:** I hope that, in that new world, a new sun will rise, bringing one dawn after another to all.

> **白厄：**希望在那个新世界里，会有一轮崭新的朝阳升起——为所有人带去一个又一个黎明。

**Trinnon:** ...

> **缇宁：**……

**Trinnon:** Snowy, you've grown into the person Agy always dreamed of.

> **缇宁：**你已经长成了阿雅期望中的样子呀，小白。

**Trinnon:** Indeed, we don't need to say goodbye here...

> **缇宁：**的确，我们不需要在这里道别……

**Trinnon:** Because, even without the prophecy, we have the confidence to say... you will become that rising sun.

> **缇宁：**因为，无需预言的确证，*我们*也有自信断言…你一定会成为那一轮朝阳。

**Trinnon:** And all of us... will see each other again "tomorrow."

> **缇宁：**而我们所有人…都一定会在「明天」再度相见。

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Mydei:** How...

> **万敌：**你…

**Trinnon:** All shall bid farewell to one...

> **？？？：**众人将与一人离别……

**Trinnon:** And that person alone will witness the miracle...

> **？？？：**惟其人将觐见奇迹……

**Trinnon:** ...Such is destiny.

> **？？？：**…此乃命运使然。

**Mydei:** Become the dawn... Deliverer.

> **万敌：**成为黎明吧…救世主。

*(Cutscene ends)*

Herta's Projection: ...

Herta's Projection: Burning skies, shattered statues, corpses strewn everywhere... What a mess. If this is what the inside of "Amphoreus" looks like, it's truly shocking.

Herta's Projection: To be honest, I was initially skeptical. But right now, I'm starting to see your point...

Herta's Projection: Screwy.

Screwllum's Projection: If that's the case, Herta, do you now have enough reason to believe my conjecture about the true nature of "Amphoreus"?

Herta's Projection: Let's not expand on that topic just yet. That Intellitron — Lygus, was it? He's not completely dead. That was probably a fake body. Something behind it... is still defying us.

Screwllum's Projection: I must admit, even if both of us worked together, it wouldn't be a simple task piercing through its defenses.

Screwllum's Projection: For now, we can only observe this world as bystanders.

Herta's Projection: I knew we should've brought Stephen along.

Herta's Projection: No wonder the Garden of Recollection wanted to work with the Astral Express — without the power of the Trailblaze, it'd be impossible to unearth this place's secrets. But in the end, the Garden emerged unscathed, and it was the Nameless who got burnt. Curiosity definitely killed Aki.

Herta's Projection: That big guy over there — the black ball spewing meteors — you think it's got anything to do with the Lord Ravager?

Screwllum's Projection: Alas, without sufficient computational power in the stream projection, I am unable to analyze the Path Energy.

Screwllum's Projection: Suggestion: The immediate priority now is to find the whereabouts of the two Nameless. Research priority on the Destruction can be lowered. Every second counts, before that Intellitron's preventative measures kick in.

Herta's Projection: All right, all right, stop making a fuss. How many times have we collaborated, and when did it ever not end in a perfect outcome?

Herta's Projection: Why not add another mission objective while we're at it: The card-playing Memokeeper told me that the frozen Trailblazer girl might just also be trapped inside Amphoreus.

Screwllum's Projection: If time allows, I will also investigate March 7th's whereabouts.

Screwllum's Projection: ...

Screwllum's Projection: Herta, do you hear that? A wave of desperate cries from the heart of the mountain city. The lives there are undergoing intense suffering.

Screwllum's Projection: Conclusion: The butterfly has already flapped its wings, and an impending storm closes in. The Nameless are important allies, and you and I have matters to attend to, Herta. I beseech you...

Screwllum's Projection: In moments when a choice needs to be made, one must not be distracted due to personal interests.

Herta's Projection: ...

Herta's Projection: Of course I know that.

Herta's Projection: Tsk, fate really is a game of dice, Screwy. Who'd have thought? That thing once sowed chaos across the cosmos, causing the Intelligentsia Guild to splinter and disintegrate. It wasn't until I cracked the solitary waves theory that its lingering effects were put to an end.

Herta's Projection: Yet, in a forgotten corner of the cosmos, one slipped through the net and remained operational. And it was this machine that eventually became the cradle for the birth of a certain Lord Ravager...

Herta's Projection: The source of Amphoreus' tragedy...

Herta's Projection: ...is an Emperor's Scepter that survived to this day.

**█████:** ...

**█████:** This is... what the world looked like originally?

**█████:** And what are you doing here?

> **黑塔的投影：**翁法罗斯悲剧的源头……

**█████:** ...Answer my question, Lygus.

> **黑塔的投影：**是一台残存至今的「帝皇权杖」啊。

**Lygus:** Ah... Another hero has arrived at the Vortex. I can no longer remember how many times this has happened.

> **来古士：**啊…涡心再次迎来了一位英雄。我已记不清这是第几次了。

**Lygus:** The author of each epic, as the prophecy has foretold, stays their pen at this point.

> **来古士：**每一篇史诗的作者，均如那既定的预言所书，在此处封笔。

**Lygus:** Just like each cycle of the extrapolation, in accordance to THEIR primordial design, ends here.

> **来古士：**正如每一世演算，均如祂原初的设计那般，在此处终结。

**Lygus:** No need to panic, Lord Phainon. I stepped into this space purely for one purpose: To witness the prophecy's Miracle of Genesis.

> **来古士：**不必紧张，白厄阁下。我踏入此间，只是为了一个纯粹的目的：亲眼见证神谕中「创世的奇迹」。

**█████:** ...You've never expressed interest in the Flame-Chase Journey. When my companions and I ran, bled, and sacrificed for the Coreflames, you never so much as lifted a finger.

> **█████：**…你从未展现过对逐火之旅的兴趣。在我和同伴们为火种奔走、流血、牺牲之时，你也从未伸出过援手。

**█████:** An indifferent observer, an Antikytheran who is neutral till the end... Why would you suddenly express concern for the Chrysos Heirs' mission?

> **█████：**一介冰冷的旁观者，将「中立」贯彻到底的安提基色拉人…怎就突然关心起了黄金裔的使命？

**Lygus:** When that lone swordmaster left everything behind, plunging into a sea of madness, I was on the shore, listening. When that mighty lord shed his armor, filling the crevices of the earth with his iron bones, I was on the mountain top, gazing down.

> **来古士：**那孤独的剑士告别一切，只身浸入疯狂之海时，我就在岸边聆听；那伟岸的霸主卸下战甲，以铁骨填平大地的裂隙时，我就在山巅俯瞰。

**Lygus:** Across a thousand years of the Flame-Chase Journey, I have witnessed the ardor, resolve, division, and unity of the Chrysos Heirs with mine own eyes...

> **来古士：**跨越千年的逐火征程，我将汝等黄金裔的苦难、决意、分裂、团结尽收眼中……

**Lygus:** Just as you were all born to shoulder the guidance of the prophecy, I too, having bathed in the gaze of distant stars, have seen the deepest, darkest of destinies...

> **来古士：**正如你们生来背负神谕的指引，在沐浴来自遥远星辰的视线之后，我同样看见了至深至暗的命运……

**Lygus:** I will become the most loyal reader of the epics, a spectator voraciously consuming the valorous deeds of you heroes.

> **来古士：**我将成为史诗最忠诚的读者，将汝等英雄伟业尽收眼底的观众。

**█████:** ...

> **█████：**……

**█████:** "The gaze of distant stars"...?

> **█████：**「遥远星辰的视线」…？

**Lygus:** One day, you will understand. But not now.

> **来古士：**终有一日，你会理解的。但不是现在。

**Lygus:** At this moment, please disregard this observer, and finish what you must, Lord Phainon.

> **来古士：**此刻，请忽略我这位旁观者，去完成你必须完成的事业吧，白厄阁下。

**█████:** ...

> **█████：**……

**█████:** I once believed that as long as I chased the flames relentlessly, the fog before me would clear, and fate would reveal its truest form.

> **█████：**我曾坚信，只要不断追逐火光，前路的迷雾就会散去，命运会展现它最真诚的样子。

**█████:** But along the way, we... they, all who passed, never once received the justice promised by the prophecy.

> **█████：**但这一路走来，我们…他们，所有逝去的人，从未获得神谕应允的公正。

**Lygus:** I have perceived: Outside the Vortex, the world is collapsing, and the children of humanity are crying.

> **来古士：**我听到了：涡心之外，世界在崩塌，人子在哭嚎。

**Lygus:** But you, Lord Phainon, will bury the old world with that Coreflame, bringing all things into a gray unknown...

> **来古士：**而你，白厄阁下，将用那枚火种埋葬旧世，将万物带入一片灰色的未知——

**█████:** It doesn't matter. This cruel Flame-Chase Journey has made me abandon my fantasies. The future is not a paradise with a gentle west wind, waiting for us to step in...

> **█████：**——无妨。残酷的逐火已经让我抛弃了幻想，未来不可能是一片沐浴着西风的理想乡，静候着我们踏入其中……

**█████:** If what lies ahead is a mass of chaos, I will tear it apart...

> **█████：**如果等在前方的是一团混沌，那就由我将它撕裂……

**█████:** ...Then usher in the first ray of blazing sunlight.

> **█████：**…再引入第一缕烈阳的光芒。

**Lygus:** All shall bid farewell to one, and that person alone will witness the miracle. Such is the will of fate.

> **来古士：**众人将与一人离别，惟其人将觐见奇迹，此乃命运使然。

**Lygus:** Yet this time, will an epic with endless cycles...

> **来古士：**然而这一次，无限轮回的史诗……

**█████:** ...Is it you?

> **█████：**…是你吗？

**Lygus:** ...

> **来古士：**……

**Lygus:** ...turn a new page?

> **来古士：**要翻开新的一页了么？

**???:** ...Of course.

> **？？？：**…当然。

**???:** This will be a romantic story like none that has come before...

> **？？？：**这一定是个不同以往的浪漫故事……

**Cyrene:** You think so too, right?

> **昔涟：**你也是这么想的，对吧？

### Post-Mission

【游戏内短信 · Everyone, the train car that...】

**Pom-Pom:** Everyone, the train car that entered Amphoreus is still out of contact

> **帕姆：**各位，进入翁法罗斯的车厢依然处于失联状态

**Himeko:** March 7th's condition also seems to be getting worse

> **姬子：**三月七的症状看上去也愈发恶劣了

**Himeko:** How's the situation on your end, Welt?

> **姬子：**瓦尔特，你那边情况怎么样？

**Welt:** Things are going well so far. Herta invited Screwllum to assist us, and we had a detailed chat in the seclusion zone.

> **瓦尔特：**进展还算顺利，黑塔邀请螺丝咕姆助阵，我们在禁闭舱段仔细聊了聊

**Welt:** Both geniuses contributed with their knowledge. We're now closer to clearing up the mystery surrounding Amphoreus.

> **瓦尔特：**两位天才见多识广，现在离拨开翁法罗斯的迷雾又近了一步

**Welt:** However... there's a lot of unexpected information. It's hard to explain everything in a text message.

> **瓦尔特：**不过…有不少意料之外的信息，以短信恐怕难以陈述清楚

**Welt:** I'll send you the meeting notes as a video right now.

> **瓦尔特：**我马上将会议记录以影像的形式传输给你

**Himeko:** Understood

> **姬子：**了解
